print("eunc v5 by kolenvlogger | https://discord.gg/WCZghenRpw")
local BENCH_VERSION = "v5"

local SHOW_FAILURES = true

local results = {
	pass = 0,
	fail = 0,
	total = 0,
	failures = {},
}

local function add(name, ok, reason)
	results.total = results.total + 1
	if ok then
		results.pass = results.pass + 1
	else
		results.fail = results.fail + 1
		table.insert(results.failures, { name = name, reason = reason or "returned false" })
	end
end

local function check(name, fn)
	local ok, result = pcall(fn)

	if not ok then
		add(name, false, tostring(result))
		return
	end

	if result == true then
		add(name, true)
	elseif type(result) == "string" then
		add(name, false, result)
	else
		add(name, false, "check returned " .. tostring(result))
	end
end

local function near(a, b, eps)
	if type(a) ~= "number" or type(b) ~= "number" then
		return false
	end
	if a ~= a or b ~= b then
		return false
	end
	return math.abs(a - b) <= (eps or 0.01)
end

local function drop(...)
	for index = 1, select("#", ...) do
		local inst = select(index, ...)

		if inst ~= nil then
			pcall(function()
				inst:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end
end

local function enumValues(name)
	local map = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(Enum[name]:GetEnumItems()) do
		map[item.Name] = item.Value
	end

	return map
end

check("1 inherited BasePart members resolve on Part", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	if type(part.Transparency) ~= "number" then
		return "Transparency on Part came back as " .. type(part.Transparency)
	end
	if typeof(part.CFrame) ~= "CFrame" then
		return "CFrame on Part came back as " .. typeof(part.CFrame)
	end
	if typeof(part.Size) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "Size on Part came back as " .. typeof(part.Size)
	end
	if typeof(part.Color) ~= "Color3" then
		return "Color on Part came back as " .. typeof(part.Color)
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("2 IsA accepts every superclass in the chain", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	if not part:IsA("BasePart") then
		return "Part is not a BasePart"
	end
	if not part:IsA("PVInstance") then
		return "Part is not a PVInstance"
	end
	if not part:IsA("Instance") then
		return "Part is not an Instance"
	end

	drop(part)

	local model = Instance.new("Model")

	if not model:IsA("PVInstance") then
		return "Model is not a PVInstance"
	end

	drop(model)
	return true
end)

check("3 IsA rejects unrelated classes", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local bad = {}

	for _, class in ipairs({ "Model", "Folder", "GuiObject", "Humanoid", "DataModel" }) do
		if part:IsA(class) then
			table.insert(bad, class)
		end
	end

	drop(part)

	if #bad > 0 then
		return "Part claims to be " .. table.concat(bad, ", ")
	end

	return true
end)

check("4 IsA is case-sensitive", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	if not part:IsA("Part") then
		return "Part does not match Part"
	end
	if part:IsA("part") or part:IsA("PART") or part:IsA("basepart") then
		return "class matching ignores case"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("5 Part.Shape belongs to Enum.PartType", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local shape = part.Shape

	if typeof(shape) ~= "EnumItem" then
		drop(part)
		return "Shape came back as " .. typeof(shape)
	end
	if shape.EnumType ~= Enum.PartType then
		drop(part)
		return "Shape.EnumType is not Enum.PartType"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("6 EnumItem identity across access paths", function()
	local byDot = Enum.PartType.Block
	local byIndex = Enum.PartType["Block"]
	local byList

	for _, item in ipairs(Enum.PartType:GetEnumItems()) do
		if item.Name == "Block" then
			byList = item
			break
		end
	end

	if byDot ~= byIndex then
		return "dot access and bracket access give different objects"
	end
	if byDot ~= byList then
		return "GetEnumItems gives a different object than dot access"
	end
	if byDot.Value ~= 1 then
		return "Block should be 1, got " .. tostring(byDot.Value)
	end

	return true
end)

check("7 Enum.Material keeps real non-contiguous values", function()
	if Enum.Material.Plastic.Value ~= 256 then
		return "Plastic should be 256, got " .. tostring(Enum.Material.Plastic.Value)
	end
	if Enum.Material.Neon.Value ~= 288 then
		return "Neon should be 288, got " .. tostring(Enum.Material.Neon.Value)
	end
	if Enum.Material.Wood.Value ~= 512 then
		return "Wood should be 512, got " .. tostring(Enum.Material.Wood.Value)
	end

	return true
end)

check("8 an enum property keeps only its own type of item", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	local stored = part.Shape

	if typeof(stored) ~= "EnumItem" then
		drop(part)
		return "Shape came back as " .. typeof(stored)
	end
	if stored.EnumType ~= Enum.PartType then
		drop(part)
		return "Shape came back holding an item from a different enum"
	end
	if stored ~= Enum.PartType.Cylinder then
		drop(part)
		return "Shape did not keep the item that was set"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("9 number properties keep only numbers", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Transparency = 0.25
	part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 3, 4)

	local transparency = part.Transparency
	local size = part.Size

	drop(part)

	if type(transparency) ~= "number" then
		return "Transparency came back as " .. type(transparency)
	end
	if not near(transparency, 0.25, 0.0001) then
		return "Transparency did not keep 0.25"
	end
	if typeof(size) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "Size came back as " .. typeof(size)
	end
	if size ~= Vector3.new(2, 3, 4) then
		return "Size did not keep the vector that was set"
	end

	return true
end)

check("10 Model:GetBoundingBox returns CFrame plus size", function()
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(4, 1, 2)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
	part.Parent = model
	model.Parent = workspace

	local boxCFrame, boxSize = model:GetBoundingBox()

	if typeof(boxCFrame) ~= "CFrame" then
		drop(model)
		return "first return value is " .. typeof(boxCFrame) .. ", expected CFrame"
	end
	if typeof(boxSize) ~= "Vector3" then
		drop(model)
		return "second return value is " .. typeof(boxSize) .. ", expected Vector3"
	end
	if not near((boxSize - Vector3.new(4, 1, 2)).Magnitude, 0) then
		drop(model)
		return "box size is " .. tostring(boxSize) .. ", expected 4, 1, 2"
	end

	drop(model)
	return true
end)

check("11 Model pivot round-trip moves the contents", function()
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	part.Parent = model
	model.PrimaryPart = part
	model.Parent = workspace

	local target = CFrame.new(10, 20, 30)

	model:PivotTo(target)

	local pivot = model:GetPivot()
	local moved = (part.Position - target.Position).Magnitude

	drop(model)

	if typeof(pivot) ~= "CFrame" then
		return "GetPivot returned " .. typeof(pivot)
	end
	if not near((pivot.Position - target.Position).Magnitude, 0, 0.05) then
		return "pivot did not move to the target position"
	end
	if not near(moved, 0, 0.05) then
		return "the part inside the model did not move with it"
	end

	return true
end)

check("12 FindFirstChildWhichIsA versus FindFirstChildOfClass", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local mesh = Instance.new("MeshPart")

	mesh.Parent = folder

	local byIsa = folder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
	local byClass = folder:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
	local byExact = folder:FindFirstChildOfClass("MeshPart")

	drop(folder)

	if byIsa ~= mesh then
		return "FindFirstChildWhichIsA missed the MeshPart for BasePart"
	end
	if byClass ~= nil then
		return "FindFirstChildOfClass matched a subclass, it should only match the exact class"
	end
	if byExact ~= mesh then
		return "FindFirstChildOfClass missed MeshPart"
	end

	return true
end)

check("13 Instance.new refuses NotCreatable classes", function()
	for _, class in ipairs({ "DataModel", "Workspace", "Terrain" }) do
		if pcall(Instance.new, class) then
			return "Instance.new allowed " .. class
		end
	end

	local ok, camera = pcall(Instance.new, "Camera")

	if not ok or typeof(camera) ~= "Instance" then
		return "Instance.new refused Camera, which is creatable"
	end

	drop(camera)
	return true
end)

check("14 GetAttributeChangedSignal is scoped to its attribute", function()
	local inst = Instance.new("Folder")
	local hitA, hitB = false, false
	local connA = inst:GetAttributeChangedSignal("a"):Connect(function()
		hitA = true
	end)
	local connB = inst:GetAttributeChangedSignal("b"):Connect(function()
		hitB = true
	end)

	inst:SetAttribute("a", 1)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while not hitA and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		connA:Disconnect()
		connB:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(inst)

	if not hitA then
		return "the signal for attribute a never fired"
	end
	if hitB then
		return "changing attribute a fired the signal for attribute b"
	end

	return true
end)

check("15 gui members resolve through the superclass chain", function()
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	if typeof(label.Position) ~= "UDim2" then
		drop(label)
		return "Position on TextLabel came back as " .. typeof(label.Position)
	end
	if typeof(label.Size) ~= "UDim2" then
		drop(label)
		return "Size on TextLabel came back as " .. typeof(label.Size)
	end
	if typeof(label.AnchorPoint) ~= "Vector2" then
		drop(label)
		return "AnchorPoint came back as " .. typeof(label.AnchorPoint)
	end
	if type(label.ZIndex) ~= "number" then
		drop(label)
		return "ZIndex came back as " .. type(label.ZIndex)
	end
	if not label:IsA("GuiObject") or not label:IsA("GuiBase2d") then
		drop(label)
		return "TextLabel is not recognised as a GuiObject / GuiBase2d"
	end

	drop(label)
	return true
end)

check("16 absolute gui sizes are Vector2", function()
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 45)

	local okSize = typeof(frame.AbsoluteSize) == "Vector2"
	local okPos = typeof(frame.AbsolutePosition) == "Vector2"

	drop(frame)

	if not okSize then
		return "AbsoluteSize is not a Vector2"
	end
	if not okPos then
		return "AbsolutePosition is not a Vector2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("17 Enum.CameraType has the newer items", function()
	local items = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(Enum.CameraType:GetEnumItems()) do
		items[item.Name] = item.Value
	end

	if items.Scriptable ~= 6 then
		return "Scriptable should be 6, got " .. tostring(items.Scriptable)
	end
	if items.Orbital ~= 7 then
		return "Orbital should be 7, got " .. tostring(items.Orbital)
	end
	if items.Custom ~= 5 then
		return "Custom should be 5, got " .. tostring(items.Custom)
	end

	return true
end)

check("18 Camera.NearPlaneZ is read only and negative", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local value = camera.NearPlaneZ

	if type(value) ~= "number" then
		return "NearPlaneZ came back as " .. type(value)
	end
	if value >= 0 then
		return "NearPlaneZ should be negative, got " .. tostring(value)
	end

	local wrote = pcall(function()
		camera.NearPlaneZ = -1
	end)

	if wrote and camera.NearPlaneZ == -1 then
		return "NearPlaneZ accepted a write, it is marked read only"
	end

	return true
end)

check("19 HumanoidStateType has the real value gaps", function()
	local items = {}
	local count = 0

	for _, item in ipairs(Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems()) do
		count = count + 1
		items[item.Name] = item.Value
	end

	if count ~= 17 then
		return "expected 17 items, got " .. count
	end
	if items.Running ~= 8 then
		return "Running should be 8, got " .. tostring(items.Running)
	end
	if items.RunningNoPhysics ~= 10 then
		return "RunningNoPhysics should be 10, got " .. tostring(items.RunningNoPhysics)
	end
	if items.Dead ~= 15 then
		return "Dead should be 15, got " .. tostring(items.Dead)
	end
	if items.None ~= 18 then
		return "None should be 18, got " .. tostring(items.None)
	end

	return true
end)

check("20 TweenInfo keeps everything it was built with", function()
	local info = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, 1, true, 0.5)

	if not near(info.Time, 0.25, 0.0001) then
		return "Time came back as " .. tostring(info.Time)
	end
	if info.EasingStyle ~= Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce then
		return "EasingStyle was not kept"
	end
	if info.EasingDirection ~= Enum.EasingDirection.InOut then
		return "EasingDirection was not kept"
	end
	if info.RepeatCount ~= 1 then
		return "RepeatCount came back as " .. tostring(info.RepeatCount)
	end
	if info.Reverses ~= true then
		return "Reverses came back as " .. tostring(info.Reverses)
	end
	if not near(info.DelayTime, 0.5, 0.0001) then
		return "DelayTime came back as " .. tostring(info.DelayTime)
	end

	return true
end)

check("21 Model.PrimaryPart only takes a BasePart", function()
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	if model.PrimaryPart ~= nil then
		drop(model)
		return "PrimaryPart should start as nil"
	end

	local accepted = pcall(function()
		model.PrimaryPart = folder
	end)

	if accepted then
		drop(model)
		return "PrimaryPart accepted a Folder"
	end

	model.PrimaryPart = part

	if model.PrimaryPart ~= part then
		drop(model)
		return "PrimaryPart did not keep the part that was set"
	end

	drop(model)
	return true
end)

check("22 destroyed instances lock their parent", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Parent = workspace
	part:Destroy()

	if part.Parent ~= nil then
		return "Parent is still set after Destroy"
	end

	local wrote = pcall(function()
		part.Parent = workspace
	end)

	if wrote and part.Parent == workspace then
		return "a destroyed part could be parented again"
	end

	return true
end)

check("23 KeyCode values are the real ones", function()
	local items = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
		items[item.Name] = item.Value
	end

	if items.A ~= 97 then
		return "KeyCode.A should be 97, got " .. tostring(items.A)
	end
	if items.W ~= 119 then
		return "KeyCode.W should be 119, got " .. tostring(items.W)
	end
	if items.Space ~= 32 then
		return "KeyCode.Space should be 32, got " .. tostring(items.Space)
	end
	if items.LeftShift ~= 304 then
		return "KeyCode.LeftShift should be 304, got " .. tostring(items.LeftShift)
	end
	if items.Escape ~= 27 then
		return "KeyCode.Escape should be 27, got " .. tostring(items.Escape)
	end

	return true
end)

check("24 enum items report their own type", function()
	local item = Enum.PartType.Block

	local value = item.Value
	local name = item.Name

	if type(value) ~= "number" then
		return "Value came back as " .. type(value)
	end
	if type(name) ~= "string" then
		return "Name came back as " .. type(name)
	end
	if item.EnumType ~= Enum.PartType then
		return "the item belongs to a different enum"
	end

	return true
end)

check("25 value objects start with their real defaults", function()
	local str = Instance.new("StringValue")
	local num = Instance.new("NumberValue")
	local bool = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	local obj = Instance.new("ObjectValue")

	local okStr = str.Value == ""
	local okNum = num.Value == 0
	local okBool = bool.Value == false
	local okObj = obj.Value == nil

	drop(str, num)
	drop(bool, obj)

	if not okStr then
		return "StringValue should start empty"
	end
	if not okNum then
		return "NumberValue should start at 0"
	end
	if not okBool then
		return "BoolValue should start false"
	end
	if not okObj then
		return "ObjectValue should start nil"
	end

	return true
end)

check("26 camera priorities are spread out", function()
	local map = enumValues("RenderPriority")

	if map.First ~= 0 then
		return "First should be 0, got " .. tostring(map.First)
	end
	if map.Input ~= 100 then
		return "Input should be 100, got " .. tostring(map.Input)
	end
	if map.Camera ~= 200 then
		return "Camera should be 200, got " .. tostring(map.Camera)
	end
	if map.Character ~= 300 then
		return "Character should be 300, got " .. tostring(map.Character)
	end
	if map.Last ~= 2000 then
		return "Last should be 2000, got " .. tostring(map.Last)
	end

	if #Enum.RenderPriority:GetEnumItems() ~= 5 then
		return "expected 5 items, got " .. #Enum.RenderPriority:GetEnumItems()
	end

	return true
end)

check("27 face and axis enums keep their order", function()
	local normal = enumValues("NormalId")
	local axis = enumValues("Axis")

	if normal.Right ~= 0 or normal.Top ~= 1 or normal.Back ~= 2 then
		return "NormalId order is wrong"
	end
	if normal.Left ~= 3 or normal.Bottom ~= 4 or normal.Front ~= 5 then
		return "NormalId order is wrong"
	end
	if axis.X ~= 0 or axis.Y ~= 1 or axis.Z ~= 2 then
		return "Axis order is wrong"
	end

	return true
end)

end)

	end

		return "Bold should be 700, got " .. tostring(map.Bold)
	end

		return "Heavy should be 900, got " .. tostring(map.Heavy)
	end

		return "expected 9 items, got " .. #Enum.FontWeight:GetEnumItems()
	end

	return true
end)

check("35 tween playback states", function()

	end
	if map.Paused ~= 3 or map.Completed ~= 4 or map.Cancelled ~= 5 then
		return "PlaybackState values are wrong at the end"
	end

	return true
end)

end)

check("43 roll off modes", function()
	local map = enumValues("RollOffMode")

	if map.Inverse ~= 0 or map.Linear ~= 1 then
		return "RollOffMode values are wrong at the start"
	end
	if map.LinearSquare ~= 2 or map.InverseTapered ~= 3 then
		return "RollOffMode values are wrong at the end"
	end

	return true
end)

check("44 user input types keep the gaps", function()
	local map = enumValues("UserInputType")

	if map.MouseButton1 ~= 0 or map.MouseWheel ~= 3 or map.MouseMovement ~= 4 then
		return "UserInputType values are wrong for mouse input"
	end
	if map.Touch ~= 7 then
		return "Touch should be 7, got " .. tostring(map.Touch)
	end
	if map.Keyboard ~= 8 or map.Focus ~= 9 then
		return "UserInputType values are wrong for keyboard and focus"
	end
	if map.None ~= 22 then
		return "None should be 22, got " .. tostring(map.None)
	end

	return true
end)

check("45 mouse behaviour options", function()
	local map = enumValues("MouseBehavior")

	if map.Default ~= 0 or map.LockCenter ~= 1 or map.LockCurrentPosition ~= 2 then
		return "MouseBehavior values are wrong"
	end

	return true
end)

check("46 CameraType is accepted by the camera", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local before = camera.CameraType
	local wrote = pcall(function()
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Orbital
	end)

	if not wrote then
		return "CameraType rejected Enum.CameraType.Orbital"
	end
	if camera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Orbital then
		return "CameraType did not keep the value that was set"
	end

	pcall(function()
		camera.CameraType = before
	end)

	return true
end)

check("47 camera type only changes to a real camera type", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local before = camera.CameraType

	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Orbital
	local orbital = camera.CameraType

	camera.CameraType = before
	local restored = camera.CameraType

	if orbital ~= Enum.CameraType.Orbital then
		return "CameraType did not keep the value that was set"
	end
	if restored ~= before then
		return "CameraType did not go back to " .. tostring(before.Name)
	end

	return true
end)

check("48 camera subject takes an instance", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace

	camera.CameraSubject = part

	local read = camera.CameraSubject

	drop(part)

	if read ~= part then
		return "CameraSubject did not keep the instance that was set"
	end

	return true
end)

check("49 tween runs to completion on a number value", function()
	local service = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	holder.Value = 0
	holder.Parent = workspace

	local tween = service:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Value = 100 })

	if typeof(tween) ~= "Instance" then
		drop(holder)
		return "Create returned " .. typeof(tween) .. ", expected an Instance"
	end
	if tween.ClassName ~= "Tween" then
		drop(holder)
		return "Create returned a " .. tostring(tween.ClassName)
	end

	tween:Play()

	local deadline = os.clock() + 2
	while tween.PlaybackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	local state = tween.PlaybackState
	local value = holder.Value

	drop(holder)

	if state ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
		return "tween never reached Completed, ended in " .. tostring(state)
	end
	if not near(value, 100, 0.5) then
		return "value ended at " .. tostring(value) .. ", expected 100"
	end

	return true
end)

check("50 tween cancel stops it", function()
	local service = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	holder.Value = 0
	holder.Parent = workspace

	local tween = service:Create(holder, TweenInfo.new(5, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Value = 100 })

	tween:Play()
	task.wait()
	tween:Cancel()
	task.wait()

	local state = tween.PlaybackState
	local value = holder.Value

	drop(holder)

	if state ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Cancelled then
		return "cancelled tween reports " .. tostring(state)
	end
	if near(value, 100, 1) then
		return "cancelled tween still reached the goal value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("51 the tween keeps the info it was created with", function()
	local service = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	holder.Parent = workspace

	local info = TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	local tween = service:Create(holder, info, { Value = 5 })

	local kept = tween.TweenInfo
	local ok = kept ~= nil
		and near(kept.Time, 0.5, 0.0001)
		and kept.EasingStyle == Enum.EasingStyle.Quad
		and kept.EasingDirection == Enum.EasingDirection.Out

	drop(holder)

	if not ok then
		return "the tween did not keep the info it was created with"
	end

	return true
end)

check("52 number value keeps decimals", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	holder.Value = 12.75

	if type(holder.Value) ~= "number" then
		drop(holder)
		return "Value came back as " .. type(holder.Value)
	end
	if not near(holder.Value, 12.75, 0.0001) then
		drop(holder)
		return "NumberValue came back as " .. tostring(holder.Value)
	end

	drop(holder)
	return true
end)

check("53 int value rounds off decimals", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("IntValue")

	holder.Value = 3.7

	local value = holder.Value

	drop(holder)

	if type(value) ~= "number" then
		return "IntValue.Value is " .. type(value)
	end
	if value == 3.7 then
		return "IntValue kept a decimal, it should be whole"
	end
	if value ~= 4 and value ~= 3 then
		return "IntValue ended at " .. tostring(value)
	end

	return true
end)

check("54 cframe value holds a cframe", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("CFrameValue")
	local target = CFrame.new(1, 2, 3)

	holder.Value = target

	if typeof(holder.Value) ~= "CFrame" then
		drop(holder)
		return "CFrameValue.Value is " .. typeof(holder.Value)
	end
	if not near((holder.Value.Position - target.Position).Magnitude, 0, 0.0001) then
		drop(holder)
		return "CFrameValue did not keep the position that was set"
	end

	drop(holder)
	return true
end)

check("55 brick color value exposes number and colour", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("BrickColorValue")

	holder.Value = BrickColor.new("Really red")

	local number = holder.Value.Number
	local color = holder.Value.Color

	local ok = type(number) == "number" and typeof(color) == "Color3"

	drop(holder)

	if not ok then
		return "BrickColor did not expose Number and Color"
	end

	return true
end)

check("56 object value reports what it holds", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	holder.Value = folder

	local read = holder.Value
	local kind = typeof(read)

	drop(holder)

	if kind ~= "Instance" then
		return "Value came back as " .. kind
	end
	if read ~= folder then
		return "ObjectValue did not keep the folder that was set"
	end

	return true
end)

check("57 changed fires once per assignment", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")
	local fired = 0

	holder.Changed:Connect(function()
		fired = fired + 1
	end)

	holder.Value = 1
	task.wait()

	drop(holder)

	if fired ~= 1 then
		return "Changed fired " .. fired .. " times for one assignment, expected 1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("58 writer and reader share the same attribute slot", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part:SetAttribute("health", 50)

	if part:GetAttribute("health") ~= 50 then
		drop(part)
		return "the value written by SetAttribute did not come back"
	end

	part:SetAttribute("health", nil)

	if part:GetAttribute("health") ~= nil then
		drop(part)
		return "setting an attribute to nil did not clear it"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("59 attributes hold a mix of types", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local vector = Vector3.new(1, 2, 3)

	part:SetAttribute("text", "hello")
	part:SetAttribute("count", 7)
	part:SetAttribute("ratio", 0.5)
	part:SetAttribute("flag", true)
	part:SetAttribute("point", vector)

	local ok = part:GetAttribute("text") == "hello"
		and part:GetAttribute("count") == 7
		and near(part:GetAttribute("ratio"), 0.5, 0.0001)
		and part:GetAttribute("flag") == true
		and part:GetAttribute("point") == vector

	drop(part)

	if not ok then
		return "one of the attributes did not come back the way it went in"
	end

	return true
end)

check("60 attribute names are case sensitive", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part:SetAttribute("Health", 10)

	local ok = part:GetAttribute("Health") == 10 and part:GetAttribute("health") == nil

	drop(part)

	if not ok then
		return "attribute lookup ignored letter case"
	end

	return true
end)

check("61 attribute changed signal fires on change", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local fired = false
	local connection = part.AttributeChanged:Connect(function()
		fired = true
	end)

	part:SetAttribute("stage", 1)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while not fired and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(part)

	if not fired then
		return "AttributeChanged never fired"
	end

	return true
end)

check("62 tag round trip", function()
	local service = game:GetService("CollectionService")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	service:AddTag(part, "eunc_tag")

	if not service:HasTag(part, "eunc_tag") then
		drop(part)
		return "HasTag says the tag is missing right after AddTag"
	end

	local listed = false
	for _, tag in ipairs(service:GetTags(part)) do
		if tag == "eunc_tag" then
			listed = true
			break
		end
	end

	if not listed then
		drop(part)
		return "GetTags did not list the tag that was added"
	end

	service:RemoveTag(part, "eunc_tag")

	if service:HasTag(part, "eunc_tag") then
		drop(part)
		return "HasTag still says the tag is there after RemoveTag"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("63 adding the same tag twice keeps one", function()
	local service = game:GetService("CollectionService")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	service:AddTag(part, "eunc_dup")
	service:AddTag(part, "eunc_dup")

	local count = 0
	for _, tag in ipairs(service:GetTags(part)) do
		if tag == "eunc_dup" then
			count = count + 1
		end
	end

	drop(part)

	if count ~= 1 then
		return "the tag is listed " .. count .. " times, expected 1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("64 getting a tag list twice", function()
	local service = game:GetService("CollectionService")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	service:AddTag(part, "eunc_stable")

	local first = service:GetTags(part)
	local second = service:GetTags(part)

	local ok = #first == #second and #first >= 1

	drop(part)

	if not ok then
		return "two calls to GetTags returned different lengths"
	end

	return true
end)

check("65 GetJoints is empty with nothing attached", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace

	local lonely = part:GetJoints()

	if type(lonely) ~= "table" then
		drop(part)
		return "GetJoints returned " .. type(lonely)
	end
	if #lonely ~= 0 then
		drop(part)
		return "GetJoints found " .. #lonely .. " joints on a part with nothing attached"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("66 GetConnectedParts follows a weld", function()
	local scene = Instance.new("Folder")
	scene.Parent = workspace

	local a = Instance.new("Part")
	local b = Instance.new("Part")

	a.Anchored = true
	a.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	a.Position = Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
	a.Parent = scene

	b.Anchored = false
	b.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	b.Position = Vector3.new(1, 10, 0)
	b.Parent = scene

	local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
	weld.Part0 = a
	weld.Part1 = b
	weld.Parent = scene

	local connected = a:GetConnectedParts()

	local found = false
	for _, part in ipairs(connected) do
		if part == b then
			found = true
			break
		end
	end

	drop(scene)

	if not found then
		return "GetConnectedParts did not include the welded part"
	end

	return true
end)

check("67 part mass follows size", function()
	local small = Instance.new("Part")
	local big = Instance.new("Part")

	small.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	big.Size = Vector3.new(4, 1, 1)
	small.Anchored = true
	big.Anchored = true
	small.Parent = workspace
	big.Parent = workspace

	local smallMass = small:GetMass()
	local bigMass = big:GetMass()

	drop(small, big)

	if type(smallMass) ~= "number" or smallMass <= 0 then
		return "mass came back as " .. tostring(smallMass)
	end
	if not (bigMass > smallMass) then
		return "a part four times as wide is not heavier, masses are " .. tostring(smallMass) .. " and " .. tostring(bigMass)
	end

	return true
end)

check("68 custom physical properties are opt in", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	if part.CustomPhysicalProperties ~= nil then
		drop(part)
		return "CustomPhysicalProperties should start as nil"
	end

	local props = PhysicalProperties.new(0.7, 0.5, 0.3)

	part.CustomPhysicalProperties = props

	if typeof(part.CustomPhysicalProperties) ~= "PhysicalProperties" then
		drop(part)
		return "CustomPhysicalProperties did not keep the value that was set"
	end

	drop(part)
	return true
end)

check("69 anchored parts are fixed in place", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.15)

	local moved = (part.Position - Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)).Magnitude

	drop(part)

	if moved > 0.05 then
		return "an anchored part drifted " .. tostring(moved) .. " studs"
	end

	return true
end)

check("70 ancestors are reachable by class and by type", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	inner.Name = "Inner"
	part.Parent = inner
	inner.Parent = outer
	outer.Parent = workspace

	local byClass = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Folder")
	local byType = part:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Folder")
	local missing = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Humanoid")

	drop(outer)

	if byClass ~= inner then
		return "FindFirstAncestorOfClass did not return the closest folder"
	end
	if byType ~= inner then
		return "FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA did not return the closest folder"
	end
	if missing ~= nil then
		return "FindFirstAncestorOfClass found a Humanoid that is not there"
	end

	return true
end)

check("71 destroyed instances drop their children", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Folder")

	inner.Parent = outer
	outer.Parent = workspace

	outer:Destroy()

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while inner.Parent ~= nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	if inner.Parent ~= nil then
		return "the child still reports a parent after its parent was destroyed"
	end

	return true
end)

check("72 a name shows up in the object text", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Name = "eunc_named"

	local text = tostring(part)

	drop(part)

	if type(text) ~= "string" then
		return "tostring returned " .. type(text)
	end
	if not string.find(text, "eunc_named", 1, true) then
		return "the name is missing from tostring, got " .. text
	end

	return true
end)

check("73 find by name is exact", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Name = "Target"
	part.Parent = folder

	local exact = folder:FindFirstChild("Target")
	local wrongCase = folder:FindFirstChild("target")
	local wrongName = folder:FindFirstChild("Targ")

	drop(folder)

	if exact ~= part then
		return "FindFirstChild missed the part with the exact name"
	end
	if wrongCase ~= nil then
		return "FindFirstChild matched a different letter case"
	end
	if wrongName ~= nil then
		return "FindFirstChild matched a partial name"
	end

	return true
end)

check("74 property changed signal is per property", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local nameHits, sizeHits = 0, 0

	local a = folder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Name"):Connect(function()
		nameHits = nameHits + 1
	end)
	local b = folder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
		sizeHits = sizeHits + 1
	end)

	folder.Name = "eunc_renamed"

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while nameHits == 0 and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		a:Disconnect()
		b:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(folder)

	if nameHits == 0 then
		return "the signal for Name never fired"
	end
	if sizeHits ~= 0 then
		return "renaming the folder also fired the Parent signal"
	end

	return true
end)

check("75 humanoid starts at full health", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	local health = humanoid.Health
	local maxHealth = humanoid.MaxHealth

	drop(humanoid)

	if type(health) ~= "number" or type(maxHealth) ~= "number" then
		return "Health and MaxHealth are not both numbers"
	end
	if maxHealth <= 0 then
		return "MaxHealth started at " .. tostring(maxHealth)
	end
	if health ~= maxHealth then
		return "Health and MaxHealth do not agree at the start"
	end

	return true
end)

check("76 humanoid walk and jump defaults", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	local walk = humanoid.WalkSpeed
	local jump = humanoid.JumpPower
	local useJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower

	drop(humanoid)

	if type(walk) ~= "number" then
		return "WalkSpeed came back as " .. type(walk)
	end
	if walk <= 0 then
		return "WalkSpeed started at " .. tostring(walk)
	end
	if type(jump) ~= "number" then
		return "JumpPower came back as " .. type(jump)
	end
	if jump <= 0 then
		return "JumpPower started at " .. tostring(jump)
	end
	if type(useJumpPower) ~= "boolean" then
		return "UseJumpPower came back as " .. type(useJumpPower)
	end

	return true
end)

check("77 health cannot go above max health", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	humanoid.Health = 500

	local health = humanoid.Health

	drop(humanoid)

	if health > 100 then
		return "Health went to " .. tostring(health) .. " with a MaxHealth of 100"
	end

	return true
end)

check("78 TakeDamage removes health", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	humanoid:TakeDamage(25)

	local health = humanoid.Health

	drop(humanoid)

	if not near(health, 75, 0.01) then
		return "Health is " .. tostring(health) .. " after 25 damage, expected 75"
	end

	return true
end)

check("79 HealthChanged reports the new value", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")
	local seen

	local before = humanoid.Health

	local connection = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(value)
		seen = value
	end)

	humanoid:TakeDamage(30)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while seen == nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(humanoid)

	if seen == nil then
		return "HealthChanged never fired"
	end
	if not near(seen, before - 30, 0.01) then
		return "HealthChanged reported " .. tostring(seen) .. " after 30 damage from " .. tostring(before)
	end

	return true
end)

	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	drop(humanoid)

	if typeof(state) ~= "EnumItem" then
		return "GetState returned " .. typeof(state)
	end
	if state.EnumType ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType then
		return "the state belongs to a different enum"
	end

	return true
end)

check("84 humanoid signals exist", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	local diedSig = humanoid.Died
	local stateSig = humanoid.StateChanged
	local healthSig = humanoid.HealthChanged

	drop(humanoid)

	if typeof(diedSig) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" then
		return "Died is " .. typeof(diedSig)
	end
	if typeof(stateSig) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" then
		return "StateChanged is " .. typeof(stateSig)
	end
	if typeof(healthSig) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" then
		return "HealthChanged is " .. typeof(healthSig)
	end

	return true
end)

check("85 MoveTo is callable", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	if type(humanoid.MoveTo) ~= "function" then
		drop(humanoid)
		return "MoveTo is " .. type(humanoid.MoveTo)
	end

	local accepted = pcall(function()
		humanoid:MoveTo(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
	end)

	drop(humanoid)

	if not accepted then
		return "MoveTo threw on a plain Vector3"
	end

	return true
end)

check("86 jump height and jump power are linked", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")

	humanoid.JumpPower = 50
	local powerHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight

	humanoid.JumpHeight = 10
	local stored = humanoid.JumpHeight

	drop(humanoid)

	if type(powerHeight) ~= "number" or powerHeight <= 0 then
		return "JumpHeight came back as " .. tostring(powerHeight)
	end
	if not near(stored, 10, 0.02) then
		return "JumpHeight did not keep 10, got " .. tostring(stored)
	end

	return true
end)

check("87 humanoid reports playing animation tracks", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")
	local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
		return humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	end)

	drop(humanoid)

	if not ok then
		return "GetPlayingAnimationTracks threw"
	end
	if type(tracks) ~= "table" then
		return "GetPlayingAnimationTracks returned " .. type(tracks)
	end
	if #tracks ~= 0 then
		return "a fresh humanoid claims to have " .. #tracks .. " tracks playing"
	end

	return true
end)

check("88 viewport size is a size", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local size = camera.ViewportSize

	if typeof(size) ~= "Vector2" then
		return "ViewportSize is " .. typeof(size)
	end
	if size.X <= 0 or size.Y <= 0 then
		return "ViewportSize is " .. tostring(size.X) .. " by " .. tostring(size.Y)
	end

	return true
end)

check("89 camera cframe is writable", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local before = camera.CFrame
	local target = CFrame.new(5, 10, 15)

	camera.CFrame = target

	local moved = (camera.CFrame.Position - target.Position).Magnitude

	camera.CFrame = before

	if moved > 0.05 then
		return "the camera did not move to the cframe that was set"
	end

	return true
end)

check("90 world to viewport point gives a point and a flag", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local point, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))

	if typeof(point) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "first return value is " .. typeof(point)
	end
	if type(onScreen) ~= "boolean" then
		return "second return value is " .. type(onScreen)
	end

	return true
end)

check("91 screen point makes a ray", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(100, 100)

	if typeof(ray) ~= "Ray" then
		return "ScreenPointToRay returned " .. typeof(ray)
	end
	if typeof(ray.Origin) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(ray.Direction) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "the ray does not have a Vector3 origin and direction"
	end
	if ray.Direction.Magnitude <= 0 then
		return "the ray direction is zero length"
	end

	return true
end)

check("92 field of view defaults and sticks", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local start = camera.FieldOfView

	if type(start) ~= "number" then
		return "FieldOfView came back as " .. type(start)
	end

	camera.FieldOfView = 90

	local after = camera.FieldOfView

	camera.FieldOfView = start

	if start <= 0 or start > 120 then
		return "FieldOfView started outside its range at " .. tostring(start)
	end
	if math.clamp(after, 1, 120) ~= 90 and not near(after, 90, 0.5) then
		return "FieldOfView did not keep 90"
	end

	return true
end)

check("93 field of view stays inside its limits", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local start = camera.FieldOfView

	camera.FieldOfView = 500
	local high = camera.FieldOfView

	camera.FieldOfView = -50
	local low = camera.FieldOfView

	camera.FieldOfView = start

	if high > 120 then
		return "FieldOfView went to " .. tostring(high) .. ", the cap is 120"
	end
	if low < 1 then
		return "FieldOfView went to " .. tostring(low) .. ", the floor is 1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("94 viewport point also makes a ray", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(50, 50)

	if typeof(ray) ~= "Ray" then
		return "ViewportPointToRay returned " .. typeof(ray)
	end

	return true
end)

check("95 camera looks where it is pointed", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local before = camera.CFrame
	local target = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(0, 10, 0), Vector3.new(0, 10, -20))

	camera.CFrame = target

	local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector

	camera.CFrame = before

	if not near(look.Z, -1, 0.05) then
		return "LookVector is " .. tostring(look.Z) .. ", expected about -1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("96 clock time is inside a day", function()
	local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local clock = lighting.ClockTime

	if type(clock) ~= "number" then
		return "ClockTime is " .. type(clock)
	end
	if clock < 0 or clock > 24 then
		return "ClockTime is " .. tostring(clock) .. ", outside a day"
	end

	return true
end)

check("97 minutes after midnight moves the clock", function()
	local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local before = lighting.ClockTime

	lighting:SetMinutesAfterMidnight(720)
	local clock = lighting.ClockTime
	local minutes = lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()

	lighting.ClockTime = before

	if not near(clock, 12, 0.1) then
		return "720 minutes after midnight gave a ClockTime of " .. tostring(clock) .. ", expected 12"
	end
	if not near(minutes, 720, 1) then
		return "GetMinutesAfterMidnight returned " .. tostring(minutes)
	end

	return true
end)

		return "Technology is " .. typeof(technology)
	end
	if technology.EnumType ~= Enum.Technology then
		return "Technology belongs to a different enum"
	end

	return true
end)

		return "OutdoorAmbient is " .. typeof(outdoor)
	end
	if type(brightness) ~= "number" then
		return "Brightness is " .. type(brightness)
	end

	return true
end)

check("100 global shadows is a switch", function()
	local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local before = lighting.GlobalShadows

	lighting.GlobalShadows = not before
	local flipped = lighting.GlobalShadows
	lighting.GlobalShadows = before

	if flipped == before then
		return "GlobalShadows did not change when it was flipped"
	end

	return true
end)

check("101 gravity is writable and returns", function()
	local before = workspace.Gravity

	workspace.Gravity = 50
	local set = workspace.Gravity

	workspace.Gravity = before
	local after = workspace.Gravity

	if set ~= 50 then
		return "Gravity did not keep 50, it is " .. tostring(set)
	end
	if not near(after, before, 0.001) then
		return "Gravity did not go back to where it started"
	end

	return true
end)

check("102 game time only moves forward", function()
	local first = workspace.DistributedGameTime
	task.wait(0.05)
	local second = workspace.DistributedGameTime

	if type(first) ~= "number" or type(second) ~= "number" then
		return "DistributedGameTime is not a number"
	end
	if second < first then
		return "DistributedGameTime went backwards"
	end

	return true
end)

check("103 terrain is a terrain instance", function()
	local terrain = workspace.Terrain

	if terrain == nil then
		return "workspace.Terrain is nil"
	end
	if terrain.ClassName ~= "Terrain" then
		return "workspace.Terrain is a " .. tostring(terrain.ClassName)
	end
	if not terrain:IsA("BasePart") then
		return "Terrain is not a BasePart"
	end

	return true
end)

check("104 raycast hits a part in its path", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.05)

	local result = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0))

	drop(part)

	if result == nil then
		return "Raycast found nothing above a solid part"
	end
	if typeof(result) ~= "RaycastResult" then
		return "Raycast returned " .. typeof(result)
	end
	if result.Instance ~= part then
		return "Raycast hit " .. tostring(result.Instance) .. " instead of the part"
	end

	return true
end)

check("105 raycast result carries distance and normal", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.05)

	local result = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0))

	drop(part)

	if result == nil then
		return "Raycast found nothing"
	end
	if typeof(result.Position) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "RaycastResult.Position is " .. typeof(result.Position)
	end
	if typeof(result.Normal) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "RaycastResult.Normal is " .. typeof(result.Normal)
	end
	if type(result.Distance) ~= "number" or result.Distance <= 0 then
		return "RaycastResult.Distance is " .. tostring(result.Distance)
	end
	if not near(result.Distance, 19.5, 0.5) then
		return "Distance is " .. tostring(result.Distance) .. ", the top face is 19.5 away"
	end

	return true
end)

check("106 raycast can skip the part", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.05)

	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { part }

	local result = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(0, 20, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), params)

	drop(part)

	if result ~= nil and result.Instance == part then
		return "the excluded part was still hit"
	end

	return true
end)

check("107 bounds query finds a part in the box", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.05)

	local found = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(0, 30, 0), Vector3.new(10, 10, 10))
	local listed = false

	for _, hit in ipairs(found) do
		if hit == part then
			listed = true
			break
		end
	end

	drop(part)

	if type(found) ~= "table" then
		return "GetPartBoundsInBox returned " .. type(found)
	end
	if not listed then
		return "the part inside the box was not listed"
	end

	return true
end)

check("108 gravity pulls an unanchored part down", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = false
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 200, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.2)

	local moved = part.Position.Y

	drop(part)

	if moved >= 200 then
		return "the part did not fall, it is still at " .. tostring(moved)
	end

	return true
end)

check("109 a falling part has downward velocity", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = false
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 150, 0)
	part.Parent = workspace

	task.wait(0.15)

	local velocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity

	drop(part)

	if typeof(velocity) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "AssemblyLinearVelocity is " .. typeof(velocity)
	end
	if velocity.Y >= 0 then
		return "vertical velocity is " .. tostring(velocity.Y) .. ", expected below zero"
	end

	return true
end)

check("110 position and cframe agree", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace
	part.Position = Vector3.new(3, 4, 5)

	local fromPosition = part.Position
	local fromCFrame = part.CFrame.Position

	drop(part)

	if (fromPosition - fromCFrame).Magnitude > 0.001 then
		return "Position and CFrame.Position disagree"
	end
	if not near(fromPosition.Y, 4, 0.001) then
		return "Position.Y is " .. tostring(fromPosition.Y) .. ", expected 4"
	end

	return true
end)

check("111 screen gui is a layer collector", function()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

	local isLayer = gui:IsA("LayerCollector")
	local isInstance = gui:IsA("Instance")
	local enabled = gui.Enabled

	drop(gui)

	if not isLayer then
		return "ScreenGui is not a LayerCollector"
	end
	if not isInstance then
		return "ScreenGui is not an Instance"
	end
	if type(enabled) ~= "boolean" then
		return "Enabled is " .. type(enabled)
	end

	return true
end)

	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")

	local click = button.MouseButton1Click
	local activated = button.Activated

	drop(button)

	if typeof(click) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" then
		return "MouseButton1Click is " .. typeof(click)
	end
	if typeof(activated) ~= "RBXScriptSignal" then
		return "Activated is " .. typeof(activated)
	end

	return true
end)

check("113 udim2 keeps scale and offset", function()
	local size = UDim2.new(0.5, 20, 0.25, 10)

	if not near(size.X.Scale, 0.5, 0.0001) then
		return "X.Scale is " .. tostring(size.X.Scale)
	end
	if size.X.Offset ~= 20 then
		return "X.Offset is " .. tostring(size.X.Offset)
	end
	if not near(size.Y.Scale, 0.25, 0.0001) then
		return "Y.Scale is " .. tostring(size.Y.Scale)
	end
	if size.Y.Offset ~= 10 then
		return "Y.Offset is " .. tostring(size.Y.Offset)
	end

	return true
end)

check("114 udim2 from scale builds the same value", function()
	local a = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	local b = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)

	local ok = near(a.X.Scale, b.X.Scale, 0.0001)
		and a.X.Offset == b.X.Offset
		and near(a.Y.Scale, b.Y.Scale, 0.0001)
		and a.Y.Offset == b.Y.Offset

	if not ok then
		return "fromScale and new disagree"
	end

	return true
end)

check("115 udim2 adds component by component", function()
	local sum = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 20) + UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)

	if sum.X.Offset ~= 15 then
		return "X offset came out as " .. tostring(sum.X.Offset) .. ", expected 15"
	end
	if sum.Y.Offset ~= 25 then
		return "Y offset came out as " .. tostring(sum.Y.Offset) .. ", expected 25"
	end

	return true
end)

		return "BackgroundTransparency is " .. type(transparency)

		return "BackgroundTransparency did not keep 0.5"
	end

	return true
end)

check("118 label text and text size", function()
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	label.Text = "hello"
	label.TextSize = 24

	local text = label.Text
	local size = label.TextSize

	drop(label)

	if text ~= "hello" then
		return "Text did not keep its value"
	end
	if size ~= 24 then
		return "TextSize did not keep 24"
	end

	return true
end)

check("119 absolute size follows offset size", function()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")

	if playerGui then
		gui.Parent = playerGui
	end

	frame.Parent = gui
	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 40)
	task.wait()

	local first = frame.AbsoluteSize

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 40)
	task.wait()

	local second = frame.AbsoluteSize

	drop(frame, gui)

	if not near(first.X, 100, 1) then
		return "AbsoluteSize.X is " .. tostring(first.X) .. " for a 100 wide frame"
	end
	if not (second.X > first.X) then
		return "AbsoluteSize did not grow when the frame got wider"
	end

	return true
end)

	if hex ~= 26 then
		return "tonumber on 0x1A gave " .. tostring(hex)
	end
	if padded ~= 5 then
		return "tonumber on padded text gave " .. tostring(padded)
	end

	return true
end)

	local index = string.find("a.b", ".", 1, true)

	if index ~= 2 then
		return "find with plain matching gave " .. tostring(index)
	end

	return true
end)

check("125 gsub returns the count it replaced", function()
	local text, count = string.gsub("aaa", "a", "b")

	if text ~= "bbb" then
		return "gsub produced " .. tostring(text)
	end
	if count ~= 3 then
		return "gsub counted " .. tostring(count) .. " replacements, expected 3"
	end

	return true
end)

	local length = #"é"

	if length ~= 2 then
		return "the length of a two byte character came back as " .. tostring(length)
	end

	return true
end)

check("127 byte and char round trip", function()
	local first = string.byte("A")
	local back = string.char(65)

	if first ~= 65 then
		return "string.byte on A gave " .. tostring(first)
	end
	if back ~= "A" then
		return "string.char on 65 gave " .. tostring(back)
	end

	return true
end)

check("128 sort with a comparator", function()
	local items = { 3, 1, 2, 5, 4 }

	table.sort(items, function(a, b)
		return a > b
	end)

	if items[1] ~= 5 then
		return "the first item is " .. tostring(items[1]) .. ", expected 5"
	end
	if items[5] ~= 1 then
		return "the last item is " .. tostring(items[5]) .. ", expected 1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("129 insert and remove move the right items", function()
	local items = { "a", "b", "c" }

	table.insert(items, 2, "x")

	if items[2] ~= "x" or items[3] ~= "b" then
		return "insert put items in the wrong place"
	end

	local removed = table.remove(items, 1)

	if removed ~= "a" then
		return "remove gave back " .. tostring(removed) .. ", expected a"
	end
	if #items ~= 3 then
		return "the list has " .. #items .. " items after insert and remove, expected 3"
	end

	return true
end)

check("130 concat joins with a separator", function()
	local text = table.concat({ "a", "b", "c" }, "-")

	if text ~= "a-b-c" then
		return "concat gave " .. tostring(text)
	end

	return true
end)

check("131 unpack spreads every value", function()
	local count = select("#", table.unpack({ 1, 2, 3 }))

	if count ~= 3 then
		return "unpack produced " .. tostring(count) .. " values"
	end

	local a, b, c = table.unpack({ 7, 8, 9 })

	if a ~= 7 or b ~= 8 or c ~= 9 then
		return "unpack returned the wrong values"
	end

	return true
end)

check("132 ipairs stops at the first hole", function()
	local items = { 1, 2, nil, 4 }
	local counted = 0

	for _ in ipairs(items) do
		counted = counted + 1
	end

	local total = 0
	for _ in pairs(items) do
		total = total + 1
	end

	if counted ~= 2 then
		return "ipairs walked " .. counted .. " items, expected 2"
	end
	if total ~= 3 then
		return "pairs walked " .. total .. " keys, expected 3"
	end

	return true
end)

check("133 newindex catches unknown writes", function()
	local store = {}
	local seenKey

	local proxy = setmetatable({}, {
		__newindex = function(_, key, value)
			seenKey = key
			store[key] = value
		end,
	})

	proxy.alpha = 1

	if seenKey ~= "alpha" then
		return "__newindex was not called for the write"
	end
	if rawget(proxy, "alpha") ~= nil then
		return "the value landed on the table as well"
	end
	if store.alpha ~= 1 then
		return "the value did not reach the handler"
	end

	return true
end)

check("134 rawget skips the metatable", function()
	local proxy = setmetatable({}, {
		__index = function()
			return "from metatable"
		end,
	})

	local throughMeta = proxy.missing
	local raw = rawget(proxy, "missing")

	if throughMeta ~= "from metatable" then
		return "plain lookup did not use __index"
	end
	if raw ~= nil then
		return "rawget returned " .. tostring(raw) .. " on an empty table"
	end

	return true
end)

check("135 errors carry the message that was raised", function()
	local ok, message = pcall(function()
		error("something specific")
	end)

	if ok then
		return "error did not stop the call"
	end
	if type(message) ~= "string" then
		return "the error value is " .. type(message)
	end
	if not string.find(message, "something specific", 1, true) then
		return "the message is missing the raised text, got " .. message
	end

	return true
end)

check("136 infinity and pi are real", function()
	if math.huge <= 0 then
		return "math.huge is not above zero"
	end
	if not near(math.pi, 3.14159, 0.001) then
		return "math.pi is " .. tostring(math.pi)
	end
	if 1 / 0 ~= math.huge then
		return "dividing by zero does not give infinity"
	end

	return true
end)

check("137 math helpers behave", function()
	if math.max(3, 9, 2) ~= 9 then
		return "math.max picked the wrong value"
	end
	if math.min(3, 9, 2) ~= 2 then
		return "math.min picked the wrong value"
	end
	if math.floor(-2.5) ~= -3 then
		return "math.floor(-2.5) gave " .. tostring(math.floor(-2.5))
	end
	if math.ceil(-2.5) ~= -2 then
		return "math.ceil(-2.5) gave " .. tostring(math.ceil(-2.5))
	end

	return true
end)

check("138 modulo keeps the divisor sign", function()
	if -7 % 3 ~= 2 then
		return "-7 modulo 3 gave " .. tostring(-7 % 3) .. ", expected 2"
	end
	if 7 % -3 ~= -2 then
		return "7 modulo -3 gave " .. tostring(7 % -3) .. ", expected -2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("139 fmod truncates while modulo floors", function()
	if math.fmod(-7, 3) ~= -1 then
		return "math.fmod(-7, 3) gave " .. tostring(math.fmod(-7, 3)) .. ", expected -1"
	end
	if -7 % 3 ~= 2 then
		return "-7 modulo 3 gave " .. tostring(-7 % 3) .. ", expected 2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("140 comparison of mixed types does not crash", function()
	local ok = pcall(function()
		return 1 < "2"
	end)

	if ok then
		return "comparing a number with text slipped through"
	end

	return true
end)

check("141 type names are the plain ones", function()
	local checks = {
		{ type(nil), "nil" },
		{ type(true), "boolean" },
		{ type(12), "number" },
		{ type("s"), "string" },
		{ type({}), "table" },
		{ type(print), "function" },
		{ type(coroutine.create(function() end)), "thread" },
	}

	for _, pair in ipairs(checks) do
		if pair[1] ~= pair[2] then
			return "type returned " .. tostring(pair[1]) .. " where " .. pair[2] .. " was expected"
		end
	end

	return true
end)

check("142 Path2D carries control points and samples the curve", function()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 200)
	frame.Parent = gui

	local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

	if player then
		local playerGui = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		if playerGui then
			gui.Parent = playerGui
		end
	end

	if Path2DControlPoint == nil then
		drop(gui)
		return "Path2DControlPoint does not exist"
	end

	local path = Instance.new("Path2D")
	path.Parent = frame

	path:SetControlPoints({
		Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(0, 100)),
		Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(200, 100)),
	})

	local points = path:GetControlPoints()

	if type(points) ~= "table" then
		drop(gui)
		return "GetControlPoints returned " .. type(points)
	end
	if #points ~= 2 then
		drop(gui)
		return "SetControlPoints stored " .. #points .. " points, expected 2"
	end

	local startPos = path:GetPositionOnCurve(0)
	local midPos = path:GetPositionOnCurve(0.5)
	local endPos = path:GetPositionOnCurve(1)

	if typeof(startPos) ~= "UDim2" then
		drop(gui)
		return "GetPositionOnCurve returned " .. typeof(startPos)
	end

	local function offsetX(point)
		return (point.X.Scale * 200) + point.X.Offset
	end

	local startX = offsetX(startPos)
	local midX = offsetX(midPos)
	local endX = offsetX(endPos)

	local tangent = path:GetTangentOnCurve(0.5)

	path:UpdateControlPoint(2, Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(300, 100)))

	local moved = path:GetControlPoints()[2]
	local movedX = moved.Position.X.Offset

	frame:Destroy()
	drop(gui)

	if not near(startX, 0, 12) then
		return "the curve starts at " .. tostring(startX) .. ", expected about 0"
	end
	if not near(endX, 200, 12) then
		return "the curve ends at " .. tostring(endX) .. ", expected about 200"
	end
	if not (midX > startX and midX < endX) then
		return "the halfway point is not between the ends"
	end
	if typeof(tangent) ~= "Vector2" then
		return "GetTangentOnCurve returned " .. typeof(tangent)
	end
	if tangent.Magnitude <= 0 then
		return "the tangent is zero length on a straight line"
	end
	if not near(movedX, 300, 1) then
		return "UpdateControlPoint left the second point at " .. tostring(movedX)
	end

	return true
end)

check("143 CFrame point conversions round-trip", function()
	local frame = CFrame.new(10, 20, 30)

	local worldPoint = frame:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(1, 2, 3))

	if typeof(worldPoint) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "PointToWorldSpace returned " .. typeof(worldPoint)
	end
	if not near(worldPoint.X, 11, 0.001) or not near(worldPoint.Y, 22, 0.001) or not near(worldPoint.Z, 33, 0.001) then
		return "the point landed at " .. tostring(worldPoint.X) .. ", " .. tostring(worldPoint.Y) .. ", " .. tostring(worldPoint.Z) .. " instead of 11, 22, 33"
	end

	local back = frame:PointToObjectSpace(worldPoint)

	if not near((back - Vector3.new(1, 2, 3)).Magnitude, 0, 0.001) then
		return "PointToObjectSpace did not undo PointToWorldSpace"
	end

	local turned = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
	local rotated = turned:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))

	if typeof(rotated) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "a rotated frame returned " .. typeof(rotated)
	end

	return true
end)

check("144 second Path2D keeps its properties", function()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 200)
	frame.Parent = gui

	local path = Instance.new("Path2D")
	path.Parent = frame

	path.Closed = true
	path.Thickness = 3
	path.Visible = false
	path.ZIndex = 5

	local closed = path.Closed
	local thickness = path.Thickness
	local visible = path.Visible
	local zIndex = path.ZIndex

	local maxPoints = path:GetMaxControlPoints()

	path:SetControlPoints({
		Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)),
		Path2DControlPoint.new(UDim2.fromOffset(100, 0)),
	})

	local count = #path:GetControlPoints()

	frame:Destroy()
	drop(gui)

	if closed ~= true then
		return "Closed did not stay true"
	end
	if thickness ~= 3 then
		return "Thickness came back as " .. tostring(thickness)
	end
	if visible ~= false then
		return "Visible did not stay false"
	end
	if zIndex ~= 5 then
		return "ZIndex came back as " .. tostring(zIndex)
	end
	if type(maxPoints) ~= "number" or maxPoints <= 0 then
		return "GetMaxControlPoints returned " .. tostring(maxPoints)
	end
	if maxPoints < count then
		return "the control point limit is below the number of points that were set"
	end
	if count ~= 2 then
		return "GetControlPoints reported " .. count .. " points, expected 2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("145 RotationCurve keys and removal", function()
	local curve = Instance.new("RotationCurve")
	local turned = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(88), math.rad(68), 0)

	curve:InsertKey(RotationCurveKey.new(0, CFrame.new(), Enum.KeyInterpolationMode.Linear))
	curve:InsertKey(RotationCurveKey.new(1, turned, Enum.KeyInterpolationMode.Linear))

	local keys = curve:GetKeys()

	if type(keys) ~= "table" then
		drop(curve)
		return "GetKeys returned " .. type(keys)
	end
	if #keys ~= 2 then
		drop(curve)
		return "GetKeys reported " .. #keys .. " keys, expected 2"
	end
	if typeof(keys[2].Value) ~= "CFrame" then
		drop(curve)
		return "the key value is " .. typeof(keys[2].Value)
	end

	local sampled = curve:GetValueAtTime(0.5)

	if typeof(sampled) ~= "CFrame" then
		drop(curve)
		return "GetValueAtTime returned " .. typeof(sampled)
	end

	local right = sampled.RightVector
	local unit = typeof(right) == "Vector3" and near(right.Magnitude, 1, 0.05)

	local removed = curve:RemoveKeyAtIndex(2, 1)
	local left = #curve:GetKeys()

	drop(curve)

	if not unit then
		return "the sampled cframe does not have a unit right vector"
	end
	if removed ~= 1 then
		return "RemoveKeyAtIndex reported " .. tostring(removed) .. " removals, expected 1"
	end
	if left ~= 1 then
		return "there are " .. left .. " keys left after removing one from two"
	end

	return true
end)

check("146 cross product follows the right hand rule", function()
	local x = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local y = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)

	local z = x:Cross(y)

	if typeof(z) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "Cross returned " .. typeof(z)
	end
	if not near(z.Z, 1, 0.0001) or not near(z.X, 0, 0.0001) or not near(z.Y, 0, 0.0001) then
		return "x cross y came out as " .. tostring(z.X) .. ", " .. tostring(z.Y) .. ", " .. tostring(z.Z) .. " instead of 0, 0, 1"
	end

	local a = Vector3.new(2, 3, 4):Cross(Vector3.new(5, 6, 7))

	if not near(a.X, -3, 0.0001) or not near(a.Y, 6, 0.0001) or not near(a.Z, -3, 0.0001) then
		return "the second cross product is wrong"
	end

	local back = y:Cross(x)

	if not near(back.Z, -1, 0.0001) then
		return "swapping the inputs did not flip the result"
	end

	return true
end)

check("147 NumberRange holds both ends", function()
	local range = NumberRange.new(3, 9)

	if type(range.Min) ~= "number" then
		return "Min is " .. type(range.Min)
	end
	if not near(range.Min, 3, 0.0001) then
		return "Min is " .. tostring(range.Min)
	end
	if not near(range.Max, 9, 0.0001) then
		return "Max is " .. tostring(range.Max)
	end

	local single = NumberRange.new(5)

	if not near(single.Min, 5, 0.0001) or not near(single.Max, 5, 0.0001) then
		return "one argument did not set both ends to the same value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("148 dot product matches the angle", function()
	local a = Vector3.new(1, 2, 3)
	local b = Vector3.new(4, 5, 6)

	local product = a:Dot(b)

	if type(product) ~= "number" then
		return "Dot returned " .. type(product)
	end
	if not near(product, 32, 0.0001) then
		return "the dot product is " .. tostring(product) .. ", expected 32"
	end

	local square = Vector3.new(2, 0, 0):Dot(Vector3.new(2, 0, 0))

	if not near(square, 4, 0.0001) then
		return "a vector dotted with itself gave " .. tostring(square)
	end

	local perpendicular = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0):Dot(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))

	if not near(perpendicular, 0, 0.0001) then
		return "perpendicular vectors gave a dot of " .. tostring(perpendicular)
	end

	return true
end)

check("149 Rect holds its corners", function()
	local rect = Rect.new(10, 20, 110, 60)

	if typeof(rect.Min) ~= "Vector2" then
		return "Min is " .. typeof(rect.Min)
	end
	if typeof(rect.Max) ~= "Vector2" then
		return "Max is " .. typeof(rect.Max)
	end
	if not near(rect.Min.X, 10, 0.0001) or not near(rect.Min.Y, 20, 0.0001) then
		return "Min is " .. tostring(rect.Min.X) .. ", " .. tostring(rect.Min.Y)
	end
	if not near(rect.Max.X, 110, 0.0001) or not near(rect.Max.Y, 60, 0.0001) then
		return "Max is " .. tostring(rect.Max.X) .. ", " .. tostring(rect.Max.Y)
	end
	if not near(rect.Width, 100, 0.0001) then
		return "Width is " .. tostring(rect.Width) .. ", expected 100"
	end
	if not near(rect.Height, 40, 0.0001) then
		return "Height is " .. tostring(rect.Height) .. ", expected 40"
	end

	local fromVectors = Rect.new(Vector2.new(1, 2), Vector2.new(3, 4))

	if not near(fromVectors.Max.Y, 4, 0.0001) then
		return "building from two vectors did not keep the corners"
	end

	return true
end)

check("150 SmoothDamp eases toward the target", function()
	local service = game:GetService("TweenService")

	if type(service.SmoothDamp) ~= "function" then
		return "SmoothDamp is " .. type(service.SmoothDamp)
	end

	local value, velocity = service:SmoothDamp(0, 10, 0, 0.2, math.huge, 1 / 60)

	if type(value) ~= "number" then
		return "the first return value is " .. type(value)
	end
	if type(velocity) ~= "number" then
		return "the second return value is " .. type(velocity)
	end
	if value <= 0 then
		return "the value did not move toward the target, it is " .. tostring(value)
	end
	if value > 10 then
		return "the value overshot on the first step and reached " .. tostring(value)
	end
	if velocity <= 0 then
		return "the reported velocity is " .. tostring(velocity)
	end

	local current, speed = 0, 0

	for _ = 1, 240 do
		current, speed = service:SmoothDamp(current, 10, speed, 0.2, math.huge, 1 / 60)
	end

	if not near(current, 10, 0.5) then
		return "after 240 steps the value is " .. tostring(current) .. ", expected close to 10"
	end

	local capped = service:SmoothDamp(0, 10, 0, 0.2, 1, 1)

	if capped > 1.001 then
		return "the speed cap was ignored, the step reached " .. tostring(capped)
	end

	return true
end)

check("151 animator starts with no tracks", function()

	local animator = Instance.new("Animator")

	animator.Parent = humanoid

	local tracks = animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()

	drop(animator, humanoid)

	if type(tracks) ~= "table" then
		return "GetPlayingAnimationTracks returned " .. type(tracks)
	end
	if #tracks ~= 0 then
		return "a fresh animator already lists " .. #tracks .. " tracks"
	end

	return true
end)

check("152 animator can be looked up from its humanoid", function()
	local humanoid = Instance.new("Humanoid")
	local animator = Instance.new("Animator")

	animator.Parent = humanoid

	local found = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

	drop(animator, humanoid)

	if found == nil then
		return "the animator is not listed as a child of the humanoid"
	end
	if found ~= animator then
		return "the child lookup returned a different animator"
	end

	return true
end)

	local id = "rbxassetid://9120386436"

	sound.SoundId = id

	local kept = sound.SoundId
	local kind = typeof(kept)
	local length = sound.TimeLength

	drop(sound)

	if kind ~= "Content" and kind ~= "string" then
		return "SoundId came back as " .. kind
	end
	if tostring(kept) ~= id then
		return "SoundId came back as " .. tostring(kept)
	end
	if type(length) ~= "number" or length < 0 then
		return "TimeLength is " .. tostring(length)
	end

	return true
end)

check("157 sound methods exist", function()
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound")

	local play = sound.Play
	local stop = sound.Stop
	local pause = sound.Pause
	local resume = sound.Resume

	drop(sound)

	if type(play) ~= "function" then
		return "Play is " .. type(play)
	end
	if type(stop) ~= "function" then
		return "Stop is " .. type(stop)
	end
	if type(pause) ~= "function" and type(pause) ~= "boolean" then
		return "Pause is " .. type(pause)
	end
	if type(resume) ~= "function" then
		return "Resume is " .. type(resume)
	end

	return true
end)

check("158 bindable function answer comes back", function()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableFunction")

	bindable.OnInvoke = function(a, b)
		return a + b, a * b
	end

	local sum, product = bindable:Invoke(6, 7)

	drop(bindable)

	if sum ~= 13 then
		return "the first answer is " .. tostring(sum) .. ", expected 13"
	end
	if product ~= 42 then
		return "the second answer is " .. tostring(product) .. ", expected 42"
	end

	return true
end)

	remote.Name = "euncRemote"

check("161 unreliable remote event exists", function()
	local ok, remote = pcall(Instance.new, "UnreliableRemoteEvent")

	if not ok then
		return "Instance.new refused UnreliableRemoteEvent"
	end

	remote.Parent = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

	local fired = pcall(function()
		remote:FireServer(1, 2, 3)
	end)

	drop(remote)

	if not fired then
		return "FireServer threw on an unreliable remote"
	end

	return true
end)

check("162 bindable event keeps argument order", function()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local seen = {}

	bindable.Event:Connect(function(a, b, c)
		seen[1] = a
		seen[2] = b
		seen[3] = c
	end)

	bindable:Fire("first", 2, false)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while seen[1] == nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	drop(bindable)

	if seen[1] ~= "first" then
		return "the first argument is " .. tostring(seen[1])
	end
	if seen[2] ~= 2 then
		return "the second argument is " .. tostring(seen[2])
	end
	if seen[3] ~= false then
		return "the third argument is " .. tostring(seen[3])
	end

	return true
end)

check("163 pathfinding gives a path object", function()
	local service = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
	local ok, path = pcall(function()
		return service:CreatePath()
	end)

	if not ok then
		return "CreatePath threw: " .. tostring(path)
	end
	if typeof(path) ~= "Instance" then
		return "CreatePath returned " .. typeof(path)
	end
	if path.ClassName ~= "Path" then
		return "CreatePath returned a " .. tostring(path.ClassName)
	end

	local status = path.Status
	local waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()

	drop(path)

	if typeof(status) ~= "EnumItem" or status.EnumType ~= Enum.PathStatus then
		return "Status is not a PathStatus item"
	end
	if type(waypoints) ~= "table" then
		return "GetWaypoints returned " .. type(waypoints)
	end

	return true
end)

check("164 text size comes back as a size", function()
	local service = game:GetService("TextService")
	local ok, size = pcall(function()
		return service:GetTextSize("hello world", 14, Enum.Font.SourceSans, Vector2.new(1000, 1000))
	end)

	if not ok then
		return "GetTextSize threw: " .. tostring(size)
	end
	if typeof(size) ~= "Vector2" then
		return "GetTextSize returned " .. typeof(size)
	end
	if size.X <= 0 or size.Y <= 0 then
		return "the measured text is " .. tostring(size.X) .. " by " .. tostring(size.Y)
	end

	return true
end)
