check("167 players can be found by user id", function()
	local players = game:GetService("Players")
	local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer

	if localPlayer == nil then
		return "LocalPlayer is nil"
	end

	local found = players:GetPlayerByUserId(localPlayer.UserId)
	local ghost = players:GetPlayerByUserId(0)

	if found ~= localPlayer then
		return "GetPlayerByUserId did not return the local player"
	end
	if ghost ~= nil then
		return "GetPlayerByUserId returned something for user id 0"
	end

	return true
end)

check("168 player identity fields", function()
	local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

	if localPlayer == nil then
		return "LocalPlayer is nil"
	end

	local id = localPlayer.UserId
	local name = localPlayer.Name

	if type(id) ~= "number" or id <= 0 or id ~= math.floor(id) then
		return "UserId is " .. tostring(id)
	end
	if type(name) ~= "string" or #name == 0 then
		return "Name is " .. tostring(name)
	end

	return true
end)

check("169 the player list includes the local player", function()
	local players = game:GetService("Players")
	local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
	local list = players:GetPlayers()

	if type(list) ~= "table" then
		return "GetPlayers returned " .. type(list)
	end
	if #list == 0 then
		return "GetPlayers came back empty"
	end

	local found = false
	for _, player in ipairs(list) do
		if player == localPlayer then
			found = true
			break
		end
	end

	if not found then
		return "the list does not contain the local player"
	end

	return true
end)

check("170 model scale follows the factor", function()
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)
	part.Parent = model
	model.Parent = workspace

	local startScale = model:GetScale()
	local startSize = model:GetExtentsSize()

	model:ScaleTo(2)
	task.wait()

	local bigScale = model:GetScale()
	local bigSize = model:GetExtentsSize()

	drop(model)

	if not near(startScale, 1, 0.001) then
		return "a fresh model has a scale of " .. tostring(startScale)
	end
	if not near(bigScale, 2, 0.01) then
		return "ScaleTo(2) left the scale at " .. tostring(bigScale)
	end
	if not near(startSize.X, 2, 0.05) then
		return "the extents started at " .. tostring(startSize.X)
	end
	if not near(bigSize.X, 4, 0.1) then
		return "the extents became " .. tostring(bigSize.X) .. ", expected about 4"
	end

	return true
end)

check("171 model extents match its part", function()
	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Size = Vector3.new(3, 1, 6)
	part.Parent = model
	model.Parent = workspace

	local extents = model:GetExtentsSize()

	drop(model)

	if typeof(extents) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "GetExtentsSize returned " .. typeof(extents)
	end
	if not near(extents.X, 3, 0.05) or not near(extents.Y, 1, 0.05) or not near(extents.Z, 6, 0.05) then
		return "the extents are " .. tostring(extents.X) .. ", " .. tostring(extents.Y) .. ", " .. tostring(extents.Z)
	end

	return true
end)

check("172 json encode writes plain values", function()
	local service = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local number = service:JSONEncode(5)
	local flag = service:JSONEncode(true)
	local text = service:JSONEncode("hi")

	if number ~= "5" then
		return "encoding 5 gave " .. tostring(number)
	end
	if flag ~= "true" then
		return "encoding true gave " .. tostring(flag)
	end
	if text ~= '"hi"' then
		return "encoding a string gave " .. tostring(text)
	end

	return true
end)

check("173 json round trip keeps values", function()
	local service = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local original = { name = "eunc", count = 3, ratio = 0.5, flag = true }

	local encoded = service:JSONEncode(original)

	if type(encoded) ~= "string" then
		return "JSONEncode returned " .. type(encoded)
	end

	local decoded = service:JSONDecode(encoded)

	if type(decoded) ~= "table" then
		return "JSONDecode returned " .. type(decoded)
	end
	if decoded.name ~= "eunc" then
		return "the name came back as " .. tostring(decoded.name)
	end
	if decoded.count ~= 3 then
		return "the count came back as " .. tostring(decoded.count)
	end
	if not near(decoded.ratio, 0.5, 0.0001) then
		return "the ratio came back as " .. tostring(decoded.ratio)
	end
	if decoded.flag ~= true then
		return "the flag came back as " .. tostring(decoded.flag)
	end

	return true
end)

check("174 json escapes text properly", function()
	local service = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local original = 'quote " and newline here'

	local encoded = service:JSONEncode(original)

	if not string.find(encoded, '\\"', 1, true) then
		return "the quote was not escaped"
	end
	if string.find(encoded, "\n", 1, true) then
		return "a raw newline was left in the output"
	end

	local decoded = service:JSONDecode(encoded)

	if decoded ~= original then
		return "the text did not survive the round trip"
	end

	return true
end)

check("175 guids are unique and well shaped", function()
	local service = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local a = service:GenerateGUID(false)
	local b = service:GenerateGUID(false)
	local wrapped = service:GenerateGUID(true)

	if type(a) ~= "string" or #a == 0 then
		return "GenerateGUID returned " .. type(a)
	end
	if a == b then
		return "two calls returned the same guid"
	end
	if string.sub(wrapped, 1, 1) ~= "{" or string.sub(wrapped, -1) ~= "}" then
		return "the wrapped guid is not in braces"
	end
	if #string.gsub(a, "[^%-]", "") ~= 4 then
		return "the guid does not have four dashes, got " .. tostring(#string.gsub(a, "[^%-]", ""))
	end

	return true
end)

check("176 descendants come back parent first", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	inner.Parent = outer
	part.Parent = inner

	local list = outer:GetDescendants()
	local innerIndex, partIndex

	for index, entry in ipairs(list) do
		if entry == inner then
			innerIndex = index
		end
		if entry == part then
			partIndex = index
		end
	end

	drop(outer)

	if #list ~= 2 then
		return "GetDescendants returned " .. #list .. " entries, expected 2"
	end
	if innerIndex == nil or partIndex == nil then
		return "one of the descendants is missing from the list"
	end
	if not (innerIndex < partIndex) then
		return "the child comes before its parent in the list"
	end

	return true
end)

check("177 recursive find reaches grandchildren", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Name = "Deep"
	inner.Parent = outer
	part.Parent = inner

	local shallow = outer:FindFirstChild("Deep")
	local deep = outer:FindFirstChild("Deep", true)

	drop(outer)

	if shallow ~= nil then
		return "a plain lookup found a grandchild"
	end
	if deep ~= part then
		return "the recursive lookup missed the grandchild"
	end

	return true
end)

check("178 empty children list is a table", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local list = folder:GetChildren()

	drop(folder)

	if type(list) ~= "table" then
		return "GetChildren returned " .. type(list)
	end
	if #list ~= 0 then
		return "an empty folder reported " .. #list .. " children"
	end

	return true
end)

check("179 deferred work waits for the yield", function()
	local order = {}

	task.defer(function()
		table.insert(order, "deferred")
	end)

	table.insert(order, "inline")

	if order[2] ~= nil then
		return "the deferred function ran before the thread yielded"
	end

	task.wait()

	if order[1] ~= "inline" then
		return "the inline step is at position 1, got " .. tostring(order[1])
	end
	if order[2] ~= "deferred" then
		return "the deferred step did not run after the yield"
	end

	return true
end)

check("180 spawned work runs right away", function()
	local flag = false

	task.spawn(function()
		flag = true
	end)

	if flag ~= true then
		return "task.spawn did not run the function inline"
	end

	return true
end)

check("181 delayed work fires on time", function()
	local flag = false
	local thread = task.delay(0.05, function()
		flag = true
	end)

	if type(thread) ~= "thread" then
		return "task.delay returned " .. type(thread)
	end

	local deadline = os.clock() + 1
	while not flag and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	if not flag then
		return "the delayed function never ran"
	end

	return true
end)

check("182 cancelled work does not run", function()
	local flag = false
	local thread = task.delay(0.1, function()
		flag = true
	end)

	task.cancel(thread)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.3
	while os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	if flag then
		return "a cancelled function still ran"
	end

	return true
end)

check("183 wrapped coroutines pass values both ways", function()
	local body = coroutine.wrap(function()
		local got = coroutine.yield(1)
		return got + 1
	end)

	local first = body()
	local second = body(10)

	if first ~= 1 then
		return "the first return is " .. tostring(first) .. ", expected 1"
	end
	if second ~= 11 then
		return "the value sent back gave " .. tostring(second) .. ", expected 11"
	end

	return true
end)

check("184 coroutine status moves through its states", function()
	local co = coroutine.create(function()
		coroutine.yield()
	end)

	if coroutine.status(co) ~= "suspended" then
		return "a new coroutine reports " .. coroutine.status(co)
	end

	coroutine.resume(co)

	if coroutine.status(co) ~= "suspended" then
		return "after one yield the status is " .. coroutine.status(co)
	end

	coroutine.resume(co)

	if coroutine.status(co) ~= "dead" then
		return "a finished coroutine reports " .. coroutine.status(co)
	end

	return true
end)

	end
	if value.EnumType ~= Enum.ReverbType then
		return "AmbientReverb is from the wrong enum"
	end

	return true
end)

		["None"] = 0,
		["GeometryComplexity"] = 1,
		["Transparent"] = 2,
		["Decal"] = 3,
	}

	local items = Enum.ViewMode:GetEnumItems()

	if #items ~= 4 then
		return "ViewMode lists " .. #items .. " entries, wanted 4"
	end

	for itemName, itemValue in pairs(wanted) do
		local item = Enum.ViewMode[itemName]

		if item == nil then
			return "ViewMode has no entry called " .. itemName
		end
		if item.Value ~= itemValue then
			return "ViewMode." .. itemName .. " is " .. tostring(item.Value) .. ", not " .. tostring(itemValue)
		end
	end

	return true
end)

check("472 gui object size holds scale and offset", function()
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 40, 0.5, 8)

	local size = frame.Size

	drop(frame)

	if typeof(size) ~= "UDim2" then
		return "Size came back as " .. typeof(size)
	end
	if not near(size.X.Scale, 0.25, 0.0001) or size.X.Offset ~= 40 then
		return "the width parts did not survive"
	end
	if not near(size.Y.Scale, 0.5, 0.0001) or size.Y.Offset ~= 8 then
		return "the height parts did not survive"
	end

	return true
end)

check("473 type checks walk the whole hierarchy", function()
	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")

	local listed = {
		{ "TextButton", true },
		{ "GuiButton", true },
		{ "GuiObject", true },
		{ "GuiBase2d", true },
		{ "GuiBase", true },
		{ "Instance", true },
		{ "TextLabel", false },
		{ "Frame", false },
		{ "Folder", false },
	}

	local wrong = {}

	for _, pair in ipairs(listed) do
		if button:IsA(pair[1]) ~= pair[2] then
			table.insert(wrong, pair[1])
		end
	end

	drop(button)

	if #wrong > 0 then
		return "wrong answers for " .. table.concat(wrong, ", ")
	end

	return true
end)

check("474 instance equality is identity", function()
	local a = Instance.new("Part")
	local b = Instance.new("Part")
	local alias = a

	if a == b then
		drop(a)
		drop(b)
		return "two separate parts compare equal"
	end
	if a ~= alias then
		drop(a)
		drop(b)
		return "a part does not compare equal to itself"
	end
	if a == 5 or a == "text" then
		drop(a)
		drop(b)
		return "a part compares equal to a number or string"
	end

	drop(a, b)
	return true
end)

check("475 two parts with the same name stay separate", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local first = Instance.new("Part")
	local second = Instance.new("Part")

	first.Name = "Twin"
	second.Name = "Twin"
	first.Parent = folder
	second.Parent = folder

	local found = folder:FindFirstChild("Twin")
	local children = folder:GetChildren()

	drop(folder)

	if found ~= first then
		return "FindFirstChild did not return the first of the twins"
	end
	if #children ~= 2 then
		return "the folder reports " .. #children .. " children, expected 2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("476 a folder can be emptied and refilled", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	for index = 1, 5 do
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Name = "P" .. index
		part.Parent = folder
	end

	local before = #folder:GetChildren()

	folder:ClearAllChildren()

	local cleared = #folder:GetChildren()

	for index = 1, 2 do
		local part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Name = "R" .. index
		part.Parent = folder
	end

	local after = #folder:GetChildren()

	drop(folder)

	if before ~= 5 then
		return "the folder held " .. before .. " children, expected 5"
	end
	if cleared ~= 0 then
		return "after clearing there are still " .. cleared .. " children"
	end
	if after ~= 2 then
		return "after refilling there are " .. after .. " children, expected 2"
	end

	return true
end)

check("701 SizeConstraint numbers RelativeXY, RelativeXX and RelativeYY right", function()
	local wanted = {
		["RelativeXY"] = 0,
		["RelativeXX"] = 1,
		["RelativeYY"] = 2,
	}

	local items = Enum.SizeConstraint:GetEnumItems()

	if #items ~= 3 then
		return "SizeConstraint lists " .. #items .. " entries, wanted 3"
	end

	for itemName, itemValue in pairs(wanted) do
		local item = Enum.SizeConstraint[itemName]

		if item == nil then
			return "SizeConstraint has no entry called " .. itemName
		end
		if item.Value ~= itemValue then
			return "SizeConstraint." .. itemName .. " is " .. tostring(item.Value) .. ", not " .. tostring(itemValue)
		end
	end

	return true
end)

check("702 ActionType numbers all 5 of its entries right", function()
	local wanted = {
		["Nothing"] = 0,
		["Pause"] = 1,
		["Lose"] = 2,
		["Draw"] = 3,
		["Win"] = 4,
	}

	local items = Enum.ActionType:GetEnumItems()

	if #items ~= 5 then
		return "ActionType lists " .. #items .. " entries, wanted 5"
	end

	for itemName, itemValue in pairs(wanted) do
		local item = Enum.ActionType[itemName]

		if item == nil then
			return "ActionType has no entry called " .. itemName
		end
		if item.Value ~= itemValue then
			return "ActionType." .. itemName .. " is " .. tostring(item.Value) .. ", not " .. tostring(itemValue)
		end
	end

	return true
end)

check("703 ProximityPromptInputType numbers Keyboard, Gamepad and Touch right", function()
	local wanted = {
		["Keyboard"] = 0,
		["Gamepad"] = 1,
		["Touch"] = 2,
	}

	local items = Enum.ProximityPromptInputType:GetEnumItems()

	if #items ~= 3 then
		return "ProximityPromptInputType lists " .. #items .. " entries, wanted 3"
	end

	for itemName, itemValue in pairs(wanted) do
		local item = Enum.ProximityPromptInputType[itemName]

		if item == nil then
			return "ProximityPromptInputType has no entry called " .. itemName
		end
		if item.Value ~= itemValue then
			return "ProximityPromptInputType." .. itemName .. " is " .. tostring(item.Value) .. ", not " .. tostring(itemValue)
		end
	end

	return true
end)

check("704 UIDragDetectorBoundingBehavior numbers Automatic, EntireObject and HitPoint right", function()
	local wanted = {

check("961 writable float keeps the value", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local before = part.Transparency

	part.Transparency = 0.35
	local after = part.Transparency

	part.Transparency = before
	local restored = part.Transparency

	drop(part)

	if not near(after, 0.35, 0.0001) then
		return "Transparency read back as " .. tostring(after)
	end
	if not near(restored, before, 0.0001) then
		return "Transparency did not go back to " .. tostring(before)
	end

	return true
end)

check("962 a vector property comes back whole", function()
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
	local target = Vector3.new(1.5, 2.5, 3.5)

	mesh.Scale = target

	local read = mesh.Scale

	drop(mesh)

	if typeof(read) ~= "Vector3" then
		return "Scale read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if (read - target).Magnitude > 0.0001 then
		return "Scale came back as " .. tostring(read.X) .. ", " .. tostring(read.Y) .. ", " .. tostring(read.Z)
	end

	return true
end)

check("963 udim keeps offset and scale", function()
	local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	local target = UDim.new(0.5, 12)

	padding.PaddingTop = target

	local read = padding.PaddingTop

	drop(padding)

	if typeof(read) ~= "UDim" then
		return "PaddingTop read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if not near(read.Scale, 0.5, 0.0001) or read.Offset ~= 12 then
		return "PaddingTop came back as " .. tostring(read.Scale) .. " and " .. tostring(read.Offset)
	end

	return true
end)

check("964 grid cell size is a udim2", function()
	local grid = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
	local target = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)

	grid.CellSize = target

	local read = grid.CellSize

	drop(grid)

	if typeof(read) ~= "UDim2" then
		return "CellSize read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if read.X.Offset ~= 64 or read.Y.Offset ~= 64 then
		return "CellSize came back as " .. tostring(read.X.Offset) .. " by " .. tostring(read.Y.Offset)
	end

	return true
end)

check("965 number range property keeps both ends", function()
	local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	local target = NumberRange.new(2, 4)

	emitter.Lifetime = target

	local read = emitter.Lifetime

	drop(emitter)

	if typeof(read) ~= "NumberRange" then
		return "Lifetime read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if not near(read.Min, 2, 0.0001) or not near(read.Max, 4, 0.0001) then
		return "Lifetime came back as " .. tostring(read.Min) .. " to " .. tostring(read.Max)
	end

	return true
end)

check("966 a colour property keeps its channels", function()
	local effect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	local target = Color3.fromRGB(10, 20, 30)

	effect.TintColor = target

	local read = effect.TintColor

	drop(effect)

	if typeof(read) ~= "Color3" then
		return "TintColor read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if math.floor(read.R * 255 + 0.5) ~= 10 or math.floor(read.G * 255 + 0.5) ~= 20 then
		return "TintColor came back as a different colour"
	end

	return true
end)

check("967 an enum property takes one of its own items", function()
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom

	local x = label.TextXAlignment
	local y = label.TextYAlignment

	drop(label)

	if x ~= Enum.TextXAlignment.Center then
		return "TextXAlignment came back as " .. tostring(x)
	end
	if y ~= Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom then
		return "TextYAlignment came back as " .. tostring(y)
	end

	return true
end)

check("968 text alignment reports its alignment enum", function()
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom

	local x = label.TextXAlignment
	local y = label.TextYAlignment

	drop(label)

	if typeof(x) ~= "EnumItem" or x.EnumType ~= Enum.TextXAlignment then
		return "TextXAlignment is not a TextXAlignment item"
	end
	if typeof(y) ~= "EnumItem" or y.EnumType ~= Enum.TextYAlignment then
		return "TextYAlignment is not a TextYAlignment item"
	end
	if x ~= Enum.TextXAlignment.Center or y ~= Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom then
		return "the alignment did not keep the item that was set"
	end

	return true
end)

check("969 a cframe property keeps its position", function()
	local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
	local target = CFrame.new(1, 2, 3) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(45), 0)

	weld.C0 = target

	local read = weld.C0

	drop(weld)

	if typeof(read) ~= "CFrame" then
		return "C0 read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if (read.Position - target.Position).Magnitude > 0.0001 then
		return "C0 came back at a different position"
	end

	return true
end)

check("970 a bool property flips and flips back", function()
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
	local before = sound.Looped

	sound.Looped = not before
	local flipped = sound.Looped

	sound.Looped = before
	local restored = sound.Looped

	drop(sound)

	if flipped == before then
		return "Looped did not change"
	end
	if restored ~= before then
		return "Looped did not go back"
	end

	return true
end)

check("971 a string property keeps the exact text", function()
	local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
	local target = " type here "

	box.PlaceholderText = target

	local read = box.PlaceholderText

	drop(box)

	if read ~= target then
		return "PlaceholderText came back as " .. string.format("%q", tostring(read))
	end

	return true
end)

check("972 an int property stores whole numbers", function()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

	gui.DisplayOrder = 12
	local read = gui.DisplayOrder

	drop(gui)

	if type(read) ~= "number" then
		return "DisplayOrder read back as " .. type(read)
	end
	if read ~= 12 then
		return "DisplayOrder came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("973 a readonly property still reads", function()
	local camera = Instance.new("Camera")
	local nearPlaneZ = camera.NearPlaneZ

	if type(nearPlaneZ) ~= "number" then
		return "NearPlaneZ read back as " .. type(nearPlaneZ)
	end
	if nearPlaneZ >= 0 then
		return "NearPlaneZ is " .. tostring(nearPlaneZ) .. ", it should be negative"
	end

	return true
end)

check("974 two instances do not share property state", function()
	local first = Instance.new("Part")
	local second = Instance.new("Part")

	first.Transparency = 0.8

	local firstRead = first.Transparency
	local secondRead = second.Transparency

	drop(first, second)

	if not near(firstRead, 0.8, 0.0001) then
		return "the part that was written reads " .. tostring(firstRead)
	end
	if secondRead == 0.8 then
		return "the untouched part picked up the same value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("975 a clone copies property values", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Name = "eunc_clone"
	part.Size = Vector3.new(3, 2, 1)
	part.Transparency = 0.4

	local copy = part:Clone()

	local name = copy.Name
	local size = copy.Size
	local transparency = copy.Transparency
	local sameTable = copy == part

	drop(part, copy)

	if name ~= "eunc_clone" then
		return "the clone is called " .. tostring(name)
	end
	if (size - Vector3.new(3, 2, 1)).Magnitude > 0.0001 then
		return "the clone has a different size"
	end
	if not near(transparency, 0.4, 0.0001) then
		return "the clone has a different transparency"
	end
	if sameTable then
		return "the clone is the same object as the original"
	end

	return true
end)

check("976 setting parent shows up on both sides", function()
	local parent = Instance.new("Folder")
	local child = Instance.new("Part")

	child.Parent = parent

	local up = child.Parent
	local down = parent:FindFirstChild(child.Name)

	drop(parent)

	if up ~= parent then
		return "the child does not point at its parent"
	end
	if down ~= child then
		return "the parent does not list the child"
	end

	return true
end)

check("977 moving a child removes it from the old parent", function()
	local first = Instance.new("Folder")
	local second = Instance.new("Folder")
	local child = Instance.new("Part")

	child.Parent = first
	child.Parent = second

	local oldCount = #first:GetChildren()
	local newCount = #second:GetChildren()

	drop(first, second)

	if oldCount ~= 0 then
		return "the old parent still lists " .. oldCount .. " children"
	end
	if newCount ~= 1 then
		return "the new parent lists " .. newCount .. " children"
	end

	return true
end)

check("978 an attribute keeps its type", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part:SetAttribute("ratio", 0.25)
	part:SetAttribute("label", "x")
	part:SetAttribute("on", true)

	local ratio = part:GetAttribute("ratio")
	local label = part:GetAttribute("label")
	local on = part:GetAttribute("on")

	drop(part)

	if type(ratio) ~= "number" or not near(ratio, 0.25, 0.0001) then
		return "the number attribute came back wrong"
	end
	if label ~= "x" then
		return "the string attribute came back wrong"
	end
	if on ~= true then
		return "the boolean attribute came back wrong"
	end

	return true
end)

check("979 attributes live per instance", function()
	local first = Instance.new("Part")
	local second = Instance.new("Part")

	first:SetAttribute("tag", "one")

	local secondRead = second:GetAttribute("tag")

	drop(first, second)

	if secondRead ~= nil then
		return "the second part inherited the attribute"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1019 a camera cframe round-trips twice", function()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	if camera == nil then
		return "workspace.CurrentCamera is nil"
	end

	local before = camera.CFrame
	local first = CFrame.new(1, 1, 1)

	camera.CFrame = first
	local readFirst = camera.CFrame.Position

	camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(9, 9, 9)
	local readSecond = camera.CFrame.Position

	camera.CFrame = before

	if (readFirst - first.Position).Magnitude > 0.05 then
		return "the first cframe did not stick"
	end
	if math.abs(readSecond.X - 9) > 0.05 then
		return "the second cframe did not stick"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1022 a basepart brick colour agrees with its colour", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Really red")

	local id = part.BrickColor.Number
	local colour = part.Color
	local numbers = part.BrickColor.Number

	drop(part)

	if type(id) ~= "number" then
		return "BrickColor.Number is " .. type(id)
	end
	if typeof(colour) ~= "Color3" then
		return "Color is " .. typeof(colour)
	end
	if numbers ~= id then
		return "two reads of BrickColor.Number disagree"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1027 a motor cframe pair round-trips", function()

	local zero = CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
	local one = CFrame.new(0, 2, 0)

	motor.C0 = zero
	motor.C1 = one

	local readZero = motor.C0.Position
	local readOne = motor.C1.Position

	drop(motor)

	if math.abs(readZero.Y - 1) > 0.001 then
		return "C0 came back at y " .. tostring(readZero.Y)
	end
	if math.abs(readOne.Y - 2) > 0.001 then
		return "C1 came back at y " .. tostring(readOne.Y)
	end

	return true
end)

	local number = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	number.Value = 4.5

	local textRead = text.Value
	local numberRead = number.Value

	drop(text, number)

	if textRead ~= "abc" then
		return "the string value came back as " .. tostring(textRead)
	end
	if not near(numberRead, 4.5, 0.0001) then
		return "the number value came back as " .. tostring(numberRead)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1034 a brick colour value round-trips", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("BrickColorValue")

	holder.Value = BrickColor.new("Bright blue")

	local read = holder.Value
	local number = read.Number

	drop(holder)

	if typeof(read) ~= "BrickColor" then
		return "Value read back as " .. typeof(read)
	end
	if type(number) ~= "number" then
		return "the brick colour has no number"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1035 writing a value twice keeps the last", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")

	holder.Value = 1
	holder.Value = 2
	holder.Value = 3

	local read = holder.Value

	drop(holder)

	if read ~= 3 then
		return "Value came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1036 an int value truncates a decimal write", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("IntValue")

	holder.Value = 9.9
	local read = holder.Value

	drop(holder)

	if read == 9.9 then
		return "the decimal survived, it should be a whole number"
	end
	if read ~= 9 and read ~= 10 then
		return "Value came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1037 find first child is nil for an empty name", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	local child = Instance.new("Part")
	child.Parent = folder

	local found = folder:FindFirstChild("")

	drop(folder)

	if found ~= nil then
		return "an empty name matched something"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1038 find first child of class is nil for the wrong class", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Parent = folder

	local found = folder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Folder")

	drop(folder)

	if found ~= nil then
		return "a Folder was found inside a folder holding only a Part"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1039 a renamed parent updates the full name", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Folder")

	inner.Name = "Child"
	inner.Parent = outer
	outer.Name = "Parent"
	outer.Parent = workspace

	local before = inner:GetFullName()

	outer.Name = "Renamed"

	local after = inner:GetFullName()

	drop(outer)

	if type(before) ~= "string" then
		return "GetFullName returned " .. type(before)
	end
	if before == after then
		return "the full name did not follow the rename"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1040 a destroying parent clears the child parent", function()
	local outer = Instance.new("Folder")
	local inner = Instance.new("Part")

	inner.Parent = outer
	outer.Parent = workspace

	outer:Destroy()

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while inner.Parent ~= nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	if inner.Parent ~= nil then
		return "the child still reports a parent"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1041 a parent can be emptied one child at a time", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local first = Instance.new("Part")
	local second = Instance.new("Part")

	first.Parent = folder
	second.Parent = folder

	local both = #folder:GetChildren()

	first.Parent = nil

	local oneLeft = #folder:GetChildren()

	second.Parent = nil

	local noneLeft = #folder:GetChildren()

	drop(folder, first, second)

	if both ~= 2 then
		return "the folder started with " .. both .. " children"
	end
	if oneLeft ~= 1 then
		return "after one detach there are " .. oneLeft
	end
	if noneLeft ~= 0 then
		return "after both detach there are " .. noneLeft
	end

	return true
end)

check("1042 an instance reports its own class name", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local model = Instance.new("Model")

	local partName = part.ClassName
	local modelName = model.ClassName

	drop(part, model)

	if partName ~= "Part" then
		return "the part calls itself " .. tostring(partName)
	end
	if modelName ~= "Model" then
		return "the model calls itself " .. tostring(modelName)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1043 class name reports the class", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local model = Instance.new("Model")

	local partName = part.ClassName
	local modelName = model.ClassName

	drop(part, model)

	if type(partName) ~= "string" then
		return "ClassName came back as " .. type(partName)
	end
	if partName ~= "Part" or modelName ~= "Model" then
		return "ClassName reported " .. tostring(partName) .. " and " .. tostring(modelName)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1044 a name can be set to an empty string", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Name = ""

	local read = part.Name

	drop(part)

	if read ~= "" then
		return "Name came back as " .. string.format("%q", tostring(read))
	end

	return true
end)

check("1045 a name round-trips with spaces", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local target = "two words"

	part.Name = target

	local read = part.Name

	drop(part)

	if read ~= target then
		return "Name came back as " .. string.format("%q", tostring(read))
	end

	return true
end)

check("1046 position writes land where they were asked", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace
	part.Position = Vector3.new(11, 22, 33)

	local read = part.Position

	drop(part)

	if (read - Vector3.new(11, 22, 33)).Magnitude > 0.001 then
		return "Position came back as a different point"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1047 a cframe write moves the position too", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local target = CFrame.new(4, 5, 6)

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace
	part.CFrame = target

	local read = part.Position

	drop(part)

	if (read - target.Position).Magnitude > 0.001 then
		return "Position did not follow the cframe write"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1048 a rotation write shows in the cframe", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Anchored = true
	part.Parent = workspace
	part.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)

	local look = part.CFrame.LookVector

	drop(part)

	if not near(look.X, -1, 0.05) then
		return "LookVector.X came back as " .. tostring(look.X) .. ", expected about -1"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1049 anchoring does not change the position", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Parent = workspace
	part.Position = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)

	local before = part.Position

	part.Anchored = true

	local after = part.Position

	drop(part)

	if (after - before).Magnitude > 0.001 then
		return "anchoring moved the part"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1050 can collide flips and flips back", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local before = part.CanCollide

	part.CanCollide = not before
	local flipped = part.CanCollide

	part.CanCollide = before

	drop(part)

	if flipped == before then
		return "CanCollide did not change"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1051 massless flips and flips back", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local before = part.Massless

	part.Massless = not before
	local flipped = part.Massless

	part.Massless = before

	drop(part)

	if flipped == before then
		return "Massless did not change"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1052 a material round-trips", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	local read = part.Material

	drop(part)

	if read ~= Enum.Material.Neon then
		return "Material came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1053 a texture write shows on the decal", function()
	local decal = Instance.new("Decal")
	local id = "rbxassetid://123"

	decal.Texture = id

	local read = decal.Texture

	drop(decal)

	if tostring(read) ~= id then
		return "Texture came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1054 a tag list grows one at a time", function()
	local service = game:GetService("CollectionService")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	local empty = #service:GetTags(part)

	service:AddTag(part, "first")
	local one = #service:GetTags(part)

	service:AddTag(part, "second")
	local two = #service:GetTags(part)

	service:RemoveTag(part, "first")
	local back = #service:GetTags(part)

	drop(part)

	if empty ~= 0 then
		return "a fresh part already has " .. empty .. " tags"
	end
	if one ~= 1 then
		return "after one tag there are " .. one
	end
	if two ~= 2 then
		return "after two tags there are " .. two
	end
	if back ~= 1 then
		return "after removing one there are " .. back
	end

	return true
end)

check("1055 has tag follows add and remove", function()
	local service = game:GetService("CollectionService")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	local before = service:HasTag(part, "eunc_flip")

	service:AddTag(part, "eunc_flip")
	local during = service:HasTag(part, "eunc_flip")

	service:RemoveTag(part, "eunc_flip")
	local after = service:HasTag(part, "eunc_flip")

	drop(part)

	if before ~= false then
		return "the tag already existed"
	end
	if during ~= true then
		return "the tag was not added"
	end
	if after ~= false then
		return "the tag was not removed"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1056 an attribute clear leaves a nil read", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part:SetAttribute("gone", 1)
	local before = part:GetAttribute("gone")

	part:SetAttribute("gone", nil)
	local after = part:GetAttribute("gone")

	drop(part)

	if before ~= 1 then
		return "the attribute was not set"
	end
	if after ~= nil then
		return "the attribute survived being cleared"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1057 a value event reports the new number", function()
	local holder = Instance.new("NumberValue")
	local seen

	local connection = holder.Changed:Connect(function(value)
		seen = value
	end)

	holder.Value = 42

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while seen == nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(holder)

	if seen == nil then
		return "Changed never fired"
	end
	if seen ~= 42 then
		return "Changed reported " .. tostring(seen)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1058 a property signal reports its own property", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local seen

	local connection = folder:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Name"):Connect(function()
		seen = "name"
	end)

	folder.Name = "eunc_signal"

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while seen == nil and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	pcall(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
	end)
	drop(folder)

	if seen ~= "name" then
		return "the Name signal did not fire"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1059 a bindable event returns what the handler returns", function()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableFunction")

	bindable.OnInvoke = function(value)
		return value * 3
	end

	local result = bindable:Invoke(5)

	drop(bindable)

	if result ~= 15 then
		return "Invoke returned " .. tostring(result)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1060 a bindable event keeps the argument count", function()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local count = 0

	bindable.Event:Connect(function(...)
		count = select("#", ...)
	end)

	bindable:Fire(1, 2, 3, 4)

	local deadline = os.clock() + 0.5
	while count == 0 and os.clock() < deadline do
		task.wait()
	end

	drop(bindable)

	if count ~= 4 then
		return "the handler saw " .. count .. " arguments"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1061 a ray with a bad direction is refused", function()
	local accepted = pcall(function()
		return workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), Vector3.zero)
	end)

	if not accepted then
		return "Raycast threw on a proper direction vector"
	end

	local result = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(0, 100000, 0), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))

	if result ~= nil and typeof(result) ~= "RaycastResult" then
		return "Raycast returned " .. typeof(result)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1062 a raycast parameters filter list round-trips", function()
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { part }

	local list = params.FilterDescendantsInstances

	drop(part)

	if type(list) ~= "table" then
		return "the filter list is " .. type(list)
	end
	if #list ~= 1 or list[1] ~= part then
		return "the filter list did not keep the part"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1063 an overlap parameters filter type round-trips", function()
	local params = OverlapParams.new()

	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
	local read = params.FilterType

	if read ~= Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include then
		return "FilterType came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1064 a bounds query returns a table of parts", function()
	local asked = pcall(function()
		return workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(0, 100000, 0), Vector3.new(4, 4, 4))
	end)

	if not asked then
		return "GetPartBoundsInBox threw on a proper frame and size"
	end

	local found = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(0, 100000, 0), Vector3.new(4, 4, 4))

	if type(found) ~= "table" then
		return "GetPartBoundsInBox returned " .. type(found)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1065 a vector write does not alias the source", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local source = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)

	part.Size = source
	source = Vector3.new(9, 9, 9)

	local read = part.Size

	drop(part)

	if (read - Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)).Magnitude > 0.0001 then
		return "the part followed the variable instead of the value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1066 a cframe write does not alias the source", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local source = CFrame.new(1, 2, 3)

	part.CFrame = source
	source = CFrame.new(7, 8, 9)

	local read = part.Position

	drop(part)

	if (read - Vector3.new(1, 2, 3)).Magnitude > 0.001 then
		return "the part followed the variable instead of the value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1067 reading a property twice gives the same number", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Transparency = 0.5

	local first = part.Transparency
	local second = part.Transparency

	drop(part)

	if first ~= second then
		return "two reads gave " .. tostring(first) .. " and " .. tostring(second)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1068 reading a vector property twice gives equal values", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)

	local first = part.Size
	local second = part.Size

	drop(part)

	if first ~= second then
		return "two reads of Size disagree"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1069 an enum read twice is the same object", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	local first = part.Material
	local second = part.Material

	drop(part)

	if first ~= second then
		return "two reads gave different enum objects"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1070 a colour read twice is equal", function()
	local part = Instance.new("Part")

	part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(1, 2, 3)

	local first = part.Color
	local second = part.Color

	drop(part)

	if first ~= second then
		return "two reads of Color disagree"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1071 a clone shares no properties with the original", function()
	local first = Instance.new("Part")
	local copy = first:Clone()

	copy.Transparency = 0.9

	local originalRead = first.Transparency
	local copyRead = copy.Transparency

	drop(first, copy)

	if originalRead == 0.9 then
		return "changing the clone changed the original"
	end
	if not near(copyRead, 0.9, 0.0001) then
		return "the clone did not keep its own value"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1072 parenting after a clone keeps them apart", function()
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	local copy = part:Clone()

	part.Parent = folder

	local folderCount = #folder:GetChildren()

	drop(folder, part, copy)

	if folderCount ~= 1 then
		return "the folder lists " .. folderCount .. " children"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1073 a texture id string is not silently dropped", function()
	local texture = Instance.new("Texture")
	local id = "rbxassetid://555"

	texture.Texture = id

	local read = texture.Texture

	drop(texture)

	if tostring(read) ~= id then
		return "Texture came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1074 a gui object accepts an offset size", function()
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 100)

	local read = frame.Size

	drop(frame)

	if read.X.Offset ~= 200 or read.Y.Offset ~= 100 then
		return "Size came back as " .. tostring(read.X.Offset) .. " by " .. tostring(read.Y.Offset)
	end

	return true
end)

check("1075 a gui object accepts a scale size", function()
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	frame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.25)

	local read = frame.Size

	drop(frame)

	if not near(read.X.Scale, 0.5, 0.0001) or not near(read.Y.Scale, 0.25, 0.0001) then
		return "Size came back with different scales"
	end

	return true
end)

check("1076 a text label keeps its font", function()
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	local read = label.Font

	drop(label)

	if read ~= Enum.Font.SourceSansBold then
		return "Font came back as " .. tostring(read)
	end

	return true
end)

local rate = 0

if results.total > 0 then
	rate = results.pass / results.total * 100
end

print(("Pass: %d   Failed: %d   Total: %d   Rate: %.1f%%"):format(results.pass, results.fail, results.total, rate))

if SHOW_FAILURES and results.fail > 0 then
	print("")
	for _, failure in ipairs(results.failures) do
		print(("FAILED  %s"):format(failure.name))
		print(("        reason: %s"):format(failure.reason))
	end
end

print("eunc v5 by kolenvlogger | https://discord.gg/WCZghenRpw")

return results
